PARALLEL uses available updates more efficiently than selective baselines while retaining 94.1--99.2\% of Full-adaptation performance. Beyond multiple-choice reasoning, experiments on XSum and CNN/DailyMail show that PARALLEL retains 96.9--98.6\% of the ROUGE-1 and ROUGE-2 scores achieved by Full adaptation and 98.8--98.9\% of the corresponding ROUGE-L scores. When compared at the same cumulative adaptation time or GPU energy, PARALLEL achieves higher ARC accuracy and exhibits a more stable late-stage adaptation trajectory than Full adaptation in the representative run. These results show that learning when and how strongly to update each sample supports stable and efficient post-deployment stream adaptation while avoiding unnecessary updates.

</details>


### [100] [From Inline Notes to Collected Commentaries: Toward Context-Preserving Organization of Exegetical Knowledge in Classical Chinese Texts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29044)
*Ke Liang,Qi Su,Churen Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究将传统中国注释学中的夹注与汇编视为自然语言处理任务，提出一种保留上下文依赖性的计算框架，通过两步提示链识别注释的主文本段落和解释功能，并结合跨源提及聚类实现多版本注释整合，在《山海经》案例中达到超过97%的CoNLL F1分数，为历史注释知识的大规模组织奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 夹注和汇编评论是儒家注释传统中重要的学术交流形式，但尚未得到充分的计算研究关注。现有方法难以有效捕捉注释的上下文依赖性并实现跨版本知识整合。

Method: 采用两步提示链技术识别注释对应的主文本段落及其解释功能，并利用跨源提及聚类方法整合不同版本中的注释内容，构建统一的注释知识体系。

Result: 在《山海经》的案例研究中，该框架实现了超过97%的CoNLL F1分数，验证了其在注释识别与整合方面的高效性与准确性。

Conclusion: 该框架为历史注释知识的大规模组织提供了可行路径，可支持广泛的语文学与自然语言处理下游任务。

Abstract: Inline notes and collected commentaries are important forms of scholarly communication that evolved within the Confucian exegetical tradition, yet have received little computational attention. Drawing on traditional Chinese exegetics and philology, this paper formulates collected commentary compilation as an NLP task and proposes a computational framework that preserves the contextual dependency of inline notes while enabling their automatic compilation and exegetical knowledge organization. It combines two-step prompt chaining for identifying the associated main-text segments and exegetical functions of annotations with cross-source mention clustering for integrating commentary across editions, achieving a CoNLL F1 score above 97% in a case study on the Classic of Mountains. Our framework lays the foundation for the large-scale organization of historical exegetical knowledge, thereby supporting a broad range of downstream philological and NLP tasks.

</details>


### [101] [Tokenizer-Agnostic Engram Module](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29065)
*Jia Peng Lim,Hai Leong Chieu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出改进Deepseek Engram模块的哈希机制，通过将XOR哈希替换为通用多项式哈希，使Engram嵌入具备跨分词器的兼容性。新方法将N-gram视为从所有字节序列中采样有用片段的手段，构建统一的嵌入空间，实现字节等价序列的哈希一致性，从而在不牺牲性能的前提下提升模型复用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Engram模块依赖于基于分词器的N-gram哈希，导致不同分词器需重新训练嵌入，限制了模型的可复用性。为解决这一问题，需要设计一种对分词器不敏感的哈希机制。

Method: 将原有的XOR-based N-gram哈希替换为通用多项式哈希，并将所有N值共享一个联合嵌入空间，使字节等价的序列产生相同的哈希值，实现分词器无关的嵌入兼容性。

Result: 实验表明该方法在保持与原方法相当的性能基础上，实现了跨分词器的哈希等价性，显著提升了Engram嵌入的可迁移性和重用性。

Conclusion: 通过采用多项式哈希并构建统一嵌入空间，所提方法成功实现了Engram嵌入的分词器无关性，同时维持了原有性能水平，为大模型条件记忆模块的跨模型应用提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Deepseek's Engram, a conditional memory module, was introduced to trade-off storage versus reasoning in large language models. However, the module relies on token-level $N$-gram hashing for Engram embedding lookup, introducing a tight coupling to the tokenizer used: a model with a different tokenizer would have to train its own Engram embeddings from scratch. To improve the reusability of Engram embeddings, we propose a change to the hashing routine, enabling compatibility between Engram models using different tokenizers. Instead of modelling disjoint $N$-gram spaces, we treat $N$-gram as a method to sample potentially useful byte sequences, from all possible byte sequences across tokens. We replace the XOR-based hashing with the general polynomial hashing with a joint embedding space across $N$. This work investigates the possible trade-offs and shows that this simple substitution produces comparable performance and achieves tokenizer-agnosticism: hash equivalence for byte-equivalent token sequences.

</details>


### [102] [Semantics of Subterfuge: Benchmarking Legal Deception Detection Against General-domain State-of-the-Art](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29066)
*Theekshana Samaradiwakara,Nisansa de Silva,George C. Lobb*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文综述并比较了基于自然语言处理的自动欺骗检测（ADD）在法律领域的研究，涵盖从特征驱动的机器学习到大语言模型（LLM）的发展。通过在七个数据集（两个法律领域，五个通用领域）上对六种微调的Transformer模型和七种LLM进行统一评估，发现微调模型在数据丰富的通用领域表现优异，而少样本LLM在资源有限的法律场景中仍具竞争力；链式思维提示通常表现不佳，直接分类更优。研究强调高风险法律情境中需重视领域适应与可解释性系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欺骗检测在法律程序、执法和在线安全中具有重要意义。传统人工判断存在准确性和可扩展性局限，因此需要数据驱动的自动化方法。特别是在法律领域，亟需高效、可靠的自动欺骗检测技术以支持司法实践。

Method: 采用文献综述与对比分析方法，系统梳理NLP在自动欺骗检测中的演进路径。设计统一的实证评估框架，在七大数据集上测试六种微调Transformer模型和七种大语言模型，结合四种提示策略（如链式思维、直接分类等），全面比较其性能表现。

Result: 在数据丰富的通用领域，微调的Transformer模型表现最佳；在低资源法律领域，少样本大语言模型仍保持竞争力。链式思维提示策略普遍不如直接分类任务有效。整体表现出显著的领域敏感性。

Conclusion: 自动欺骗检测在法律应用中需重视领域适应能力与模型可解释性。未来工作应聚焦于提升模型在法律语境下的泛化能力，并开发更具透明度和可信度的系统，以满足高风险应用场景的需求。

Abstract: Deception detection has critical implications for legal proceedings, law enforcement, and online security. Although human judgment is limited in accuracy and scalability, Natural Language Processing (NLP) offers a data-driven alternative. We present a survey and comparative analysis of NLP-based Automatic Deception Detection (ADD) focusing on the legal domain, reviewing the evolution from feature-based machine learning to Large Language Model (LLM) approaches. We conduct a unified empirical evaluation across seven datasets (two legal, five general-domain), comparing six fine-tuned transformer models and seven LLMs under four prompting strategies. The results show strong domain sensitivity, with fine-tuned models excelling in data-rich general domains and few-shot LLMs remaining competitive in low-resource legal settings. Chain-of-Thought prompting often underperforms direct classification. These findings highlight the need for domain adaptation and interpretable systems in high-stakes legal contexts.

</details>


### [103] [Faster but Different: Diagnosing and Controlling Content Drift in Accelerated Multimodal Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29079)
*Yaoxuan Dou,Yang Shu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了无需训练的加速技术对基于扩散的多模态大语言模型（dMLLMs）在推理时一致性的影响。通过对比300张真实图像上加速与未加速版本的输出，发现尽管信心阈值调整不影响基础一致性，但状态刷新机制中的视觉和文本状态过时是导致生成内容漂移的关键因素。缩短KV缓存刷新间隔可实现单调的加速-一致性能权衡，并在1.3倍加速下达到近乎完全一致。该问题在其他实现如dLLM-Cache和LaViDa中也存在，但后者需同时收紧两个缓存才能恢复一致性，从而失去速度优势。独立提示和图像验证确认了阈值不敏感性和刷新恢复现象。针对性审计显示，在50对低一致性样本中有半数存在真实内容替换。双盲评估未检测到事实性错误的显著差异（95%置信区间[-0.17,+0.17]），但无法证明等效性。所有自适应或平滑刷新策略在相同计算量下均不如固定间隔策略。研究贡献在于提出一套配对诊断方法与针对特定实现的一致性控制，而非准确率或安全性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练-free加速提升了dMLLMs的部署可行性，但可能在推理过程中悄然改变生成内容，影响结果一致性，亟需系统性诊断与控制机制。

Method: 在长格式生成设置下，基于300张真实图像进行对比实验，分析不同参数配置（如信心阈值、状态刷新频率）对生成一致性的影响；通过状态刷新消融实验、图像替换干预以及独立提示/图像测试验证核心假设；采用针对性审计和双盲评估量化内容漂移与事实错误。

Result: 信心阈值调整不影响一致性，而状态刷新延迟是主要漂移来源；缩短KV缓存刷新间隔可在1.3倍加速下实现近似完全一致；其他实现（如dLLM-Cache）需双重缓存收紧才能恢复一致性，丧失速度优势；自适应或平滑刷新策略在相同计算下表现不佳；部分低一致性对中存在真实内容替换，但整体事实错误差异不显著。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了训练-free加速中隐藏的内容漂移问题，提出了基于固定间隔刷新的实现级一致性控制方案，强调其非通用准确性或安全保证，而是针对具体实现的诊断与调控工具。

Abstract: Training-free acceleration makes diffusion-based multimodal large language models (dMLLMs) more deployable, but it may silently change generated content. We study this serving-time consistency problem on 300 real images, comparing Fast-dLLM outputs with the same model's unaccelerated outputs. Across the mild parallelism induced in our long-form setting (1.05--1.25 committed tokens per step), confidence-threshold tuning changes decoding behavior but not baseline agreement. State-refresh ablations and an image-swap intervention instead identify stale visual and generated-text states as contributors to drift. For the tested Fast-dLLM implementation, shortening the KV-cache refresh interval yields a monotonic speed--agreement frontier and near-exact agreement at a measured 1.3x speedup. The initial diagnosis also appears with dLLM-Cache and LaViDa, although dLLM-Cache recovers agreement only after both caches are tightened, which removes its speed advantage. Independent prompts and images reproduce the threshold-insensitivity and refresh recovery. A targeted audit finds genuine content substitution in half of 50 low-agreement pairs. In a separate blinded two-annotator evaluation, the pooled accelerated-minus-baseline factual-error difference is 0.00 (95% CI [-0.17,+0.17]); this sample detects no difference but does not establish factual equivalence. Finally, none of the tested adaptive or smoothed-refresh variants beats the fixed interval at matched compute. Our contribution is a paired diagnostic and an implementation-scoped consistency control, not an accuracy or safety guarantee.

</details>


### [104] [Can Zero-Shot LLMs Predict Child Malnutrition? A Fairness and Temporal Robustness Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29082)
*Muhammad Ashad Kabir,Md Ahshanul Haque*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究评估了在零样本设置下使用预训练大型语言模型（LLM）GPT-4o-mini进行儿童发育迟缓预测的可行性、公平性和时间鲁棒性。基于2007至2022年孟加拉国人口健康调查数据，通过提示工程将母婴、医疗和家庭特征转化为语义可解释表示，结果显示该模型在平衡准确率上与监督学习基线相当，且对发育迟缓病例的敏感性更高，在不同性别群体中表现一致，并在各调查波次间保持稳定；但城乡及家庭财富水平间存在显著公平性差异，提示在公共卫生预测中部署基础模型前需进一步研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 儿童营养不良是低收入和中等收入国家特别是南亚地区的重要公共卫生挑战，早期识别脆弱儿童对及时干预和资源分配至关重要。传统方法依赖于有监督建模，但存在数据依赖性强、泛化能力有限等问题。利用大语言模型进行零样本预测可能提供一种无需重新训练即可快速适应新环境的新范式，有助于提升预测效率与可扩展性。

Method: 采用孟加拉国人口健康调查（BDHS）2007–2022年的数据，将母亲、儿童、医疗及家庭特征转换为基于提示（prompt-based）的语义表示；使用GPT-4o-mini在零样本设定下进行发育迟缓预测，并与随机森林模型对比性能；评估模型在不同性别、居住地、家庭财富水平等群体中的公平性，以及跨调查波次的时间鲁棒性。

Result: GPT-4o-mini在零样本设置下实现了与监督基线相当的平衡准确率，敏感性显著更高，对不同性别儿童的预测表现相对一致，且在不同调查波次间表现出稳定的预测行为；然而，在城乡和家庭财富分层中观察到明显的公平性偏差，表明模型在特定群体中可能存在系统性低估或高估。

Conclusion: 尽管预训练大语言模型在零样本条件下展现出良好的预测性能与时间鲁棒性，具备应用于公共卫生监测的巨大潜力，但其在关键社会人口学维度上的公平性缺陷不容忽视。未来应用需结合领域知识进行偏差缓解，并开展更广泛的验证，以确保其在真实世界中的安全与公平部署。

Abstract: Child malnutrition remains a major public health challenge in low- and middle-income countries, particularly in South Asia, where early identification of vulnerable children is critical for timely intervention and resource allocation. This study aims to evaluate the feasibility, fairness, and temporal robustness of using a pretrained large language model (LLM) in a zero-shot setting for child stunting prediction using population health survey data. Using Bangladesh Demographic and Health Survey (BDHS) data collected between 2007 and 2022, we transformed maternal, child, healthcare, and household characteristics into semantically interpretable prompt-based representations and evaluated GPT-4o-mini for zero-shot stunting prediction, comparing its performance against a random forest baseline and assessing fairness across demographic and socioeconomic groups as well as temporal robustness across survey waves. The results demonstrate that zero-shot inference using GPT-4o-mini achieved comparable balanced accuracy to the supervised baseline while exhibiting substantially higher sensitivity for identifying stunting cases, relatively consistent performance across child sex groups, and stable predictive behaviour across BDHS waves; however, important fairness disparities were observed across residence and household wealth categories, highlighting the need for further investigation before deployment of foundation models in public health prediction settings.

</details>


### [105] [M3-DuplexBench: A Multi-Turn, Multilingual, Multidomain Benchmark for Full-Duplex Spoken Dialogue Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29125)
*Ryo Fukuda,Atsushi Ando,Hiroki Kanagawa,Takatomo Kano,Marc Delcroix,Naohiro Tawara,Yuya Chiba*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出M3-DuplexBench，一个支持多轮、多语言、多领域的全双工对话系统基准测试，涵盖英语和日语，覆盖闲聊和多轮问答场景，并在不同对话上下文设置下评估模型表现，揭示了模型在话轮转换特性、跨语言/领域性能差距及上下文影响上的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全双工对话系统评估缺乏公平的多轮对话比较，且语言与对话领域覆盖有限，亟需一个全面、可比的基准测试以推动该领域发展。

Method: 构建M3-DuplexBench，包含多轮、多语言（英、日）、多领域（闲聊、问答）数据集，设计多种对话上下文设置（单轮、仅用户输入、教师强制全上下文），用于评估近期FDSDS模型的行为。

Result: 实验显示各模型表现出独特的话轮转换特征，跨语言和领域存在明显性能差距，对话上下文对模型表现有混合影响。

Conclusion: M3-DuplexBench为全双工对话系统提供了全面、可比较的评估框架，有助于深入理解模型行为并推动技术进步。

Abstract: Full-duplex spoken dialogue systems (FDSDSs) can listen while speaking, enabling natural behaviors such as smooth turn-taking, backchannel handling, and user barge-in handling. However, fair comparisons in multi-turn conversations remain a challenge. In addition, existing benchmarks provide limited coverage of languages and dialogue domains. We propose M3-DuplexBench, a multi-turn, multilingual, multidomain benchmark for FDSDSs. M3-DuplexBench supports English and Japanese and covers both casual conversation and multi-turn question answering. In addition, we evaluate models under multiple dialogue context settings, including single-turn, user-only, and teacher-forced full-context settings, to analyze how dialogue history affects model behavior. Experiments with recent FDSDSs reveal model-specific turn-taking characteristics, clear performance gaps across languages and domains, and mixed effects of dialogue context.

</details>


### [106] [Authorship Verification of Transcribed German-Language Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29168)
*Oren Halvani,Sophie Titze*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究德语视频字幕的语音作者身份验证（AV），探讨传统与现代方法在跨视频对识别说话人身份时的有效性。实验基于三个自建语料库（共300个视频，150位说话人）和十种AV方法，发现基于字符和词元n-gram的传统方法表现最佳（最高88%准确率，90% AUC），而基于Transformer的现代方法表现显著较差。结果表明，传统方法在语音AV任务中仍具竞争力和相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有作者身份验证研究多集中于书面文本和英语，对口语形式及非英语语言如德语关注不足。本文旨在填补这一空白，探索德语视频字幕在作者身份验证中的适用性与方法有效性。

Method: 采用十种现有的作者身份验证方法，应用于三个自建的德语视频字幕语料库，涵盖300个视频、150位说话人。通过比较传统n-gram方法与现代Transformer模型在跨视频对识别说话人身份上的表现，评估其有效性。

Result: 传统基于字符和词元n-gram的方法在所有语料库上均取得最佳性能，最高准确率达88%，AUC达90%；而基于Transformer的现代方法表现明显更差，验证了传统方法在语音AV任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: 尽管现代深度学习方法在其他领域表现出色，但在德语视频字幕的作者身份验证任务中，传统n-gram方法依然具有高度竞争力。这表明在特定数据类型和语言环境下，简单且可解释的方法仍具重要价值，为未来语音文本的作者验证研究提供了实证支持。

Abstract: Authorship Verification (AV) represents an important subfield of digital text forensics and addresses the fundamental question of whether two texts were written by the same author. Although the field has made substantial progress over the past two decades, several important challenges remain unresolved or underexplored. For instance, most AV research has focused on written texts, despite the fact that language is expressed not only in written but also in spoken form, such as in videos. Moreover, existing AV studies have predominantly concentrated on English, while other languages, including German, have received comparatively little attention. To address these research gaps, we apply AV to spoken language in the form of transcripts of German-language videos and examine the effectiveness of established AV methods in verifying a speaker's identity across video pairs. Our experimental evaluation, based on a total of ten AV methods applied to three self-compiled corpora comprising 300 videos from 150 speakers, shows that the best performance (up to 88% accuracy and 90% AUC) is achieved by traditional AV approaches based on simple character- and token n-gram representations. In contrast, more modern transformer-based approaches perform significantly worse on all evaluated corpora. Our results therefore suggest that traditional methods in the field of AV remain both competitive and relevant.

</details>


### [107] [Learning Latent Reasoning Traces for Scalar Reward Models End-to-End](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29185)
*Sanwoo Lee,Clive Bai,Hsiu-Yuan Huang,Kun Liang,Weijie Liu,Yunfang Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LatentRM提出一种新的奖励建模框架，通过将中间推理过程作为离散潜在变量来显式最大化下游标量奖励的似然性。该方法通过端到端的在线策略优化潜在推理空间，紧密耦合深度推理与精确评分，在分布内和分布外任务上均优于传统的标量、生成式及混合奖励模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统标量奖励模型依赖表面线索，泛化能力差；生成式奖励模型虽具鲁棒性但缺乏数值灵活性和概率可解释性；现有混合方法未能确保生成推理与标量奖励预测有效对齐。

Method: LatentRM将推理过程建模为离散潜在变量，通过在线策略优化直接最大化下游标量奖励的似然性，实现推理与评分的紧密耦合。

Result: 在多种任务（包括开放对话和复杂推理）上的实验表明，LatentRM在偏好建模和策略对齐方面显著优于标量、生成式及混合奖励模型，尤其在分布外场景表现更优。

Conclusion: LatentRM通过学习潜在推理表示并将其与标量奖励直接关联，实现了更稳健、可解释且高效的奖励建模，为大语言模型对齐提供了新范式。

Abstract: Reward models (RMs) are central to aligning large language models with human preferences via reinforcement learning. Although traditional scalar RMs enable efficient and probabilistic reward modeling, they rely on superficial cues that fail to generalize to complex or out-of-distribution (OOD) tasks. Conversely, generative RMs leverage extensive reasoning to improve robustness on challenging tasks, but their natural language-based scores lack the numerical flexibility and probabilistic interpretability that scalar RMs offer. While recent approaches combine both paradigms through off-policy multi-task learning, such parallel optimization does not guarantee that generated reasoning traces actively align with or benefit downstream scalar reward prediction. To address this mismatch, we propose LatentRM, a reward modeling framework that learns intermediate reasoning traces as discrete latent variables to explicitly maximize the likelihood of downstream scalar rewards. Through on-policy optimization of the latent reasoning space end-to-end, LatentRM tightly couples deep reasoning-based evaluation with precise scoring. Extensive validations on in-distribution and OOD datasets and RLHF show that LatentRM outperforms scalar, generative, and hybrid RMs on preference modeling and policy alignment across tasks ranging from open-ended conversation to complex reasoning.

</details>


### [108] [Detecting Experiential Intertextuality Across Migration Routes: Beyond Surface Similarity in French Narratives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29188)
*Sakayo Toadoum Sari,Nelly Robin,Michelle Auzanneau,Lakhdar Sais,Veronique Petit,Marie Veniard,Said Jabbour,Fabien Delorme*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出体验性互文性检测任务，旨在无标注数据情况下自动识别跨移民叙事中的共享经验。基于108份法语移民叙事，使用多种方法（词汇基线、句向量、词性结构特征、迁移主题词典、上下文感知叙事特征及零样本大模型评分）生成并评估句子对。通过816个专家标注的互文性判断进行验证，结果显示所有方法与专家判断的相关性较弱（r ≤ 0.30），其中Qwen2.5-7B零样本表现最佳（r = 0.38）。少样本提示使Mistral性能显著提升，而对Qwen则有负面影响。叙事阶段显著影响互文性，出发阶段的配对具有最强经验共鸣。结合全部31个特征的监督混合模型达到r = 0.45，相比最优单方法提升21%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移民在穿越不同地理路线（如撒哈拉以南和巴尔干走廊）时，常经历相似的创伤性事件，如警察暴力、走私者剥削、危险渡海和家庭分离。然而，现有方法难以自动识别这些跨叙事中的共享经验，尤其缺乏标注数据支持。因此，亟需一种无需人工标注即可检测迁移叙事中经验共鸣的新方法，以促进对移民集体苦难的理解与记录。

Method: 从108份法语移民叙事中自动生成句子对，采用多种无标注方法进行评分：包括词汇基线、句子嵌入、词性结构特征、迁移特定主题词典、上下文感知叙事特征，以及基于Qwen2.5-7B和Mistral-7B的零样本大模型评分（三种提示策略）。通过专家标注的816个互文性判断进行验证，并分析各方法的表现。最后构建一个融合31个特征的监督混合模型以提升性能。

Result: 所有表面、结构和嵌入方法与专家判断的相关性均较弱（r ≤ 0.30）；Qwen2.5-7B零样本方法表现最佳（r = 0.38）；少样本提示使Mistral性能大幅提升，但降低Qwen表现；叙事阶段显著预测互文性，出发阶段句子对的体验共鸣最强；最终的监督混合模型整合所有特征后达到r = 0.45，比最优单方法提升21%。

Conclusion: 尽管当前无标注方法在体验性互文性检测上仍有限，但通过结合多模态特征与大模型能力，尤其是引入叙事位置信息与监督融合机制，可显著提升识别效果。本研究为跨叙事经验共鸣的自动化分析提供了可行路径，也为理解移民集体经验提供了新工具。

Abstract: Migrants traversing geographically distinct routes such as the Trans-Saharan and Balkan corridors often recount strikingly parallel lived experiences: police violence, smuggler exploitation, dangerous crossings, and family separation. We introduce the task of experiential intertextuality detection: automatically identifying shared experiential echoes across migration narratives without requiring annotated training data. From 108 French migration narratives spanning both corridors, we automatically generate sentence pairs and score them using annotation-free methods: lexical baselines, sentence embeddings, POS-based structural features, a migration-specific theme lexicon, context-aware narrative features, and zero-shot LLM scoring with Qwen2.5-7B and Mistral-7B under three prompting strategies. We validate all methods against 816 expert-annotated intertextuality judgments (inter-annotator Krippendorff's $α= 0.27$). Our results reveal that all surface, structural, and embedding methods correlate only weakly with expert judgments ($r \leq 0.30$); Qwen2.5-7B zero-shot achieves the best single-method correlation ($r = 0.38$); few-shot examples degrade Qwen but dramatically improve Mistral; narrative position significantly predicts intertextuality, with departure-phase pairs showing the highest experiential echoes; and a supervised hybrid combining all 31 features achieves $r = 0.45$, a 21% improvement over the best individual method.

</details>


### [109] [Hy-MultiTurn: A Six-Dimensional Benchmark for Deep Multi-Turn Dialogue Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29196)
*Eileen Ye,Jiawen Tao,Yaoming Li,Chenxu Liu,Wenhan Yu,Yaxin Fan,Xiaokun Yuan,Mengzhou Wu,Yanbing Jiang,Maxm Pan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Hy-MultiTurn 是一个针对中文长轮次对话理解的基准测试，通过分析真实聊天机器人失败案例，定义了六种受控评估模式（约束记忆、精确执行、约束合成、对象定位、动作抑制、指代消解），涵盖12-76轮对话的209个任务，引入对话长度、无关话题干扰和口语化表达等挑战。评估显示当前前沿模型表现有限，即使最强的GPT-5.5也仅在41.1%的响应中完全满足要求，无模型在所有模式中表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长轮次对话基准多聚焦短对话，缺乏对中文环境下复杂多轮交互能力的充分评估，尤其在约束记忆、指代消解、动作抑制等方面存在不足，且难以揭示模型失败原因。

Method: 基于真实聊天机器人失败案例，归纳出六类常见失败机制，构建六种受控评估模式；设计209个任务，覆盖长对话、干扰信息与口语化表达，系统评估模型在复杂多轮交互中的表现。

Result: 22个前沿模型配置在该基准上表现普遍不佳，最强模型GPT-5.5仅在41.1%的任务中完全达标，无模型在全部六种模式中均表现最优，表明当前模型在深度多轮理解方面仍存在显著局限。

Conclusion: Hy-MultiTurn 为中文长轮次对话理解提供了全面、可控且具有挑战性的评估基准，揭示了当前大模型在复杂多轮交互中的关键缺陷，推动后续研究向更深层次的语境理解与推理能力发展。

Abstract: Long-running multi-turn interactions with chatbots and agents are now common, and a correct response often depends on remembering earlier details, tracking later revisions, identifying intended objects or referents, and withholding action when required conditions are unmet. Existing multi-turn benchmarks typically cover short exchanges and do not fully evaluate these capabilities in long multi-turn interactions, particularly in Chinese, while offering limited insight into how and why models fail. To address these limitations, we analyze real chatbot failures to identify six recurring mechanisms and use them to define six controlled evaluation modes in Hy-MultiTurn, a Chinese benchmark for deep multi-turn dialogue understanding. The six modes evaluate constraint memory, precise execution, constraint synthesis, object localization, action suppression, and reference resolution. Across the six modes, we construct 209 controlled tasks spanning 12-76 turns, with dialogue length, irrelevant-topic distraction, and colloquial phrasing adding further difficulty. Evaluation of 22 frontier model configurations shows that Hy-MultiTurn is broadly challenging, as even GPT-5.5, the strongest overall configuration, satisfies all requirements in only 41.1 percent of responses and no model performs best in all six modes.

</details>


### [110] [Knowing When to Quit: Diagnosing and Training LLMs to Abort Futile Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29211)
*Xinyan Guan,Jiali Zeng,Chunlei Xin,Yaojie Lu,Hongyu Lin,Xianpei Han,Le Sun,Fandong Meng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究了大语言模型在超出其能力范围的任务上产生计算昂贵但语义无效的推理现象，称为'无用推理'。通过系统分析，发现模型普遍存在能力过度扩张和行为与能力不匹配的问题，主要失败模式为表面合理但存在细微错误的伪推理。为此，提出CaRL（能力对齐强化学习）方法，通过奖励塑形激励模型拒绝无用推理，并利用事后拒绝增强将失败案例转化为拒绝监督信号。实验表明，该方法显著减少了无用推理，同时保持了不同难度任务上的性能，实现了能力对齐而不牺牲实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在处理超出自身能力的任务时，会产生看似合理但实际错误的推理过程，这种'无用推理'可能误导用户，且计算成本高，亟需一种机制来识别并避免此类行为。

Method: 提出CaRL方法，通过奖励塑形鼓励模型在不确定或超出能力范围时拒绝回答，并采用 hindsight refusal augmentation 技术，将原本失败的推理过程转化为拒绝行为的监督信号，从而实现模型行为与能力边界的对齐。

Result: 实验结果显示，CaRL有效降低了无用推理的发生率，同时在各种任务难度下均保持了良好的性能表现，证明了其在提升模型可靠性方面的有效性。

Conclusion: CaRL通过能力对齐的强化学习策略，成功抑制了大语言模型在复杂任务中的无用推理行为，使模型能够在知道何时停止推理的同时，仍保持较高的任务完成能力，为构建更可信、更安全的语言模型提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Large language models generate computationally expensive yet semantically void reasoning on beyond-capability tasks, creating risks where plausible-sounding but incorrect derivations mislead users. We characterize this \textit{futile reasoning} phenomenon through systematic analysis, revealing universal capability overreach and systematic miscalibration between capability and behavior. The dominant failure mode is specious reasoning, which outputs look superficially valid but contain subtle errors, escalating with task difficulty. To address this, we introduce \textbf{CaRL} (\textbf{Ca}pability-\textbf{a}ligned \textbf{R}einforcement \textbf{L}earning), which aligns model behavior with capability boundaries through reward shaping that incentivizes refusal over futile reasoning and hindsight refusal augmentation that converts failures into refusal supervision. Experiments demonstrate a substantial reduction in futile reasoning while preserving performance across task difficulties, effectively achieving capability-aligned behavior without sacrificing utility. \footnote{https://github.com/icip-cas/Knowing-When-to-Quit}

</details>


### [111] [Small Is Enough: Per-User Style Rewriting of AI-Edited Text via LoRA Adapters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29238)
*Antorweep Chakravorty*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: InMyStyle 是一个注重隐私的单用户系统，通过微调小规模语言模型（LoRA适配器）将AI编辑的文本重写为符合用户个人写作风格的内容，无需提示词。它利用多个本地辅助LLM生成训练数据，并支持不同长度输入的自动分块和长度感知生成。在219个科学论文评估对上，自动综合得分稳定在0.69（0-1尺度），表明小模型已足够完成任务，模型大小仅影响权衡而非质量排序。进一步评估中，5名LLM裁判对结果评分显示，InMyStyle输出的感知AI程度比原始辅助AI输入降低超过20%，且随着模型增大，感知AI程度进一步下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有文本重写系统依赖大量标注数据、需指令提示、缺乏隐私保护及难以适应个体写作风格的问题。目标是实现无提示、私密、个性化风格迁移，使重写文本更贴近用户真实写作习惯。

Method: 使用用户文档构建成对训练样本，通过多个本地小型辅助语言模型生成初始对，基于0.5B至7B参数的基座模型进行LoRA微调；引入长度感知生成预算与自动分块机制以处理异长输入。推理阶段不使用提示词，直接根据用户风格生成文本。

Result: 在219个科学论文评估对上，自动复合得分达到0.69，且在所有模型尺寸下趋于稳定，说明小模型已足够完成任务；5名裁判评价显示，输出的感知AI程度显著低于输入，且随模型规模增加而持续降低。

Conclusion: 小规模语言模型（如0.5B–7B）足以完成个性化的文本风格重写任务，模型大小主要带来性能权衡而非质量提升。InMyStyle实现了无提示、私密、高效的个性化风格迁移，在降低感知AI痕迹方面表现优异。

Abstract: InMyStyle is a privacy first, single user system that adapts small language models to rewrite AI-edited text towards an individual user's writing style without an instruction prompt at inference. Given a user's documents, it uses multiple local helper LLMs to construct paired training examples and fine tunes LoRA adapters on base models ranging from 0.5B to 7B parameters. Length aware generation budgets and automatic chunking support inputs of different lengths. On 219 evaluation pairs from a scientific-paper corpus, the automatic composite score plateaus at 0.69 [scale 0-1] across all model sizes under both greedy and sampled decoding. This observed plateau suggests that small models are sufficient for the measured rewriting task, with model size determining trade-offs rather than a stable quality ranking. As a secondary evaluation, 400 ratings from five LLM judges give InMyStyle outputs a mean perceived AI-ness score over 20% lower than their helper-AI generated inputs, while mean perceived AI-ness scores decrease with model size within InMyStyle.

</details>


### [112] [Data Turnstile: A Scalable Open Framework for Function-Calling Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29250)
*Goutham Ramakrishnan,Megha Sharma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Data Turnstile 是一个开源框架，通过用户定义的 API 规范生成高质量合成训练数据，用于提升小语言模型（SLMs）在工具调用任务中的表现。它通过分步生成、验证与错误反馈机制，实现对 API 多样性、对话复杂度和输出正确性的精细控制。实验表明，使用 Turnstile 数据微调的小模型在多个基准测试中显著超越基线，甚至接近或超过远大于其规模的大模型表现，证明了数据质量在 SLM 中的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小语言模型在工具调用任务中受限于低质量、稀疏的训练数据，而其自身无法通过容量弥补数据缺陷，因此需要高质量合成数据来突破性能瓶颈。

Method: Data Turnstile 将多轮工具调用交互分解为受控的分步生成过程，结合验证机制与错误反馈循环，自动生成符合 API 规范的高质合成数据，支持灵活控制多样性、复杂度与正确性。

Result: 在 BFCL 单轮基准上，Qwen3-0.6B 模型在无链式思维情况下达到 75.9% 准确率，接近更大模型表现；在 $τ^2$-bench 多轮代理基准上，Turnstile 微调的 Qwen3-1.7B 和 0.6B 分别提升至 31.1% 和 24.6%，分别比基线提升 4.7× 和 7×，并超越远大于其规模的模型。

Conclusion: Data Turnstile 有效解决了小语言模型在工具调用任务中因数据质量差导致的性能瓶颈，通过高质量合成数据显著提升模型能力，证明了数据质量在 SLM 中的核心地位，并释放了 1,000+ API 和 100K+ 多轮交互数据集以促进社区发展。

Abstract: Small language models (SLMs) are attractive for agentic deployment due to low latency, reduced cost, and on-device privacy, yet they struggle with tool-use tasks where training data is scarce and noisy. Unlike larger models, SLMs cannot compensate for low-quality supervision through sheer capacity, making data quality the critical bottleneck. We present Data Turnstile, an open-source framework that takes user-defined API specifications and generates high-quality synthetic training data for function calling. Turnstile decomposes multi-turn tool-use interactions into constrained, stepwise generation with validation and error-feedback loops, providing fine-grained control over API diversity, conversation complexity, and output correctness. We demonstrate effectiveness of domain adaptation with Turnstile data on two challenging function calling benchmarks. On the BFCL single-turn benchmark, a Qwen3-0.6B fine-tuned on Turnstile data without chain-of-thought achieves 75.9% overall accuracy (versus 67.4% for the base model with thinking enabled), closing the gap with thinking-enabled Qwen3-1.7B (78.4%) and Qwen3-4B (79.9%) despite being 3$\times$ and 7$\times$ smaller respectively. On $τ^2$-bench, a multi-turn agentic benchmark, Turnstile-trained Qwen3-1.7B achieves 31.1% pass^1 on the Telecom domain, improving 4.7$\times$ over its 6.6% base and surpassing Qwen2.5-32B-Instruct (27.4%), a model 19$\times$ larger. Turnstile-trained Qwen3-0.6B achieves 24.6%, improving 7$\times$ over its 3.5% base and approaching the 32B model (53$\times$ larger). We release Data Turnstile along with a dataset spanning 1,000+ APIs and 100K+ multi-turn interactions.

</details>


### [113] [CalibratedRubric: Task-Adaptive Rubric Banks for Open-Ended LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29252)
*Mengting Chen,Yanshu Sun,Wanting Liang,Beidi Luan,Rui Sun,Dezhi Chen,Jing Li,Zuo Bai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CalibratedRubric 是一种任务自适应框架，通过类型特定评分、贝叶斯可测量性过滤和基于项目反应理论（IRT）的题库构建，实现对开放式大模型输出的高效、可靠评估。它利用贝塔-伯努利一致性后验估计每个评价维度的可测量性，并采用子模信息覆盖目标生成紧凑题库，在多个领域基准测试中显著提升与人工黄金标准的一致性与排名保真度，仅需少量题库即可达到目标性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化评估方法依赖严格一致性和二元方差过滤，难以区分可测量与有信息量的评价维度，且专家标注成本高、难扩展。需要一种能自动识别并筛选有效评价维度、支持任务自适应的高效评估框架。

Method: 结合类型特定评分、贝叶斯可测量性过滤（基于Beta-Bernoulli一致后验）、以及基于项目反应理论（IRT）的题库构建；使用子模信息覆盖优化目标生成紧凑题库，实现任务自适应的评价体系。

Result: 在金融、医疗、通用和法律等多个基准上，可测量性过滤使JudgmentBench的人工黄金标准一致性从κ=0.604提升至0.743；IRT贪婪选择在所有六个响应模块中优于随机选择，仅用49个而非131个评价维度即可达成FinResearchBench决策支持任务的目标相关性；任务标签扰动进一步减少系统偏差，验证了任务自适应评分的实际意义。

Conclusion: CalibratedRubric是一种高效、具备不确定性感知能力的开放式大模型评估方法，其校准效果依赖于足够的评审冗余，为大规模、细粒度的模型评估提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Reliable evaluation of open-ended LLM outputs requires fine-grained rubrics, yet expert curation is costly and difficult to scale. Existing automated pipelines rely on strict judge unanimity and binary variance filters, which cannot distinguish measurable rubrics from informative ones. We introduce CalibratedRubric, a task-adaptive framework that combines type-specific scoring, Bayesian rubric-measurability filtering, and item response theory (IRT)-based bank assembly. CalibratedRubric estimates each rubric's measurability with a Beta--Bernoulli agreement posterior and uses a submodular information-coverage objective to construct compact rubric banks over the observed capability range. Across financial, healthcare, general, and legal benchmarks, measurability filtering improves human-gold agreement on JudgmentBench from $κ=0.604$ to $0.743$. IRT-based greedy selection improves cross-fitted rank fidelity over random selection across all six evaluated response blocks and requires only 49 rather than 131 rubrics to reach the target correlation on FinResearchBench decision-support tasks. Task-label perturbations further reduce system separation, confirming the practical relevance of task-adaptive scoring. These results support CalibratedRubric as an efficient, uncertainty-aware approach to open-ended LLM evaluation, with calibration gains depending on sufficient judge redundancy.

</details>


### [114] [Translation with Thought: Difficulty-Adaptive Reasoning via Reinforcement Learning for Multi-Domain Machine Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29287)
*Yongshi Ye,Biao Fu,Chongxuan Huang,Yidong Chen,Xiaodong Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出TwT框架，通过调节推理模式实现多领域机器翻译中的高效与高质量翻译，结合监督微调与强化学习，在多个基准上超越更大模型，减少32-60%的令牌使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多领域机器翻译中不同领域的语言复杂度差异大，需模拟人类译者根据难度调整推理努力的能力，以提升翻译质量与效率。

Method: 采用两阶段训练：第一阶段基于深度思考链（CoT）进行监督微调，使用来自DeepSeek-R1并由GPT-4o重写的人类式推理轨迹；第二阶段通过混合奖励的强化学习优化翻译质量与推理效率。

Result: 在15个基准上评估，涵盖域内、域外设置及3个已见和59个未见语言，三种主干模型上的消融实验表明，TwT-7B和TwT-14B在翻译质量上优于更大规模的SOTA推理模型，同时减少32–60%的令牌消耗。

Conclusion: 将翻译行为与认知原则对齐，可实现多领域机器翻译中的鲁棒泛化、高翻译质量与高效推理。

Abstract: Multi-domain machine translation (MDMT) poses a unique challenge due to varying levels of linguistic complexity across domains. Inspired by human translators' ability to adapt reasoning effort based on difficulty, we propose TwT (Translation with Thought), a resource-rational framework that learns to modulate inference between intuitive and deliberate reasoning. TwT is trained in two stages: (1) supervised fine-tuning on difficulty-aware long chain-of-thought traces distilled from DeepSeek-R1 and rewritten by GPT-4o to reflect human-like reasoning economy, and (2) reinforcement learning with a hybrid reward to optimize translation quality and reasoning efficiency. Evaluated on 15 benchmarks spanning in-domain and out-of-domain settings, as well as 3 seen and 59 unseen languages, with ablations across three backbone models, TwT-7B and TwT-14B outperform much larger SOTA reasoning models in translation quality, while reducing token usage by 32--60\%. These results confirm that aligning translation behavior with cognitive principles enables robust generalization, high translation quality, and efficient reasoning in MDMT.

</details>


### [115] [Cross-Lingual Transfer for Machine Translation in Turkic Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29355)
*Omer Burak Cinar,Mehmet Mert Dalkilic,Cagri Toraman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了在密切相关的突厥语族语言（土耳其语、阿塞拜疆语、乌兹别克语、哈萨克语和吉尔吉斯语）之间进行跨语言迁移的机制，通过成对的迁移矩阵分析发现，语言亲缘关系越近，迁移效果越强，尤其是土耳其语-阿塞拜疆语和哈萨克语-吉尔吉斯语之间。迁移方向具有显著影响，且同一源-目标对在不同翻译目标下表现不一。拉丁化在脚本不匹配场景中提升了BLEU和chrF得分，但效果因指标而异。此外，迁移源在不同数据集和模型设置下表现出较强的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对低资源机器翻译中的跨语言迁移行为，尤其是在密切相关的语言家族中的表现缺乏充分刻画，尤其突厥语族内部的迁移机制尚不清晰，亟需系统性分析以揭示其规律与影响因素。

Method: 采用成对迁移矩阵的方法，对五种突厥语进行两两迁移实验，每个模型使用一个源语言进行微调，并在不同目标语言上评估性能，同时保持翻译目标一致；结合mT5模型进行实验，并对比不同脚本转换（如拉丁化）的影响。

Result: 迁移效果最强的组合出现在亲缘关系较近的语言对之间，如土耳其语-阿塞拜疆语和哈萨克语-吉尔吉斯语；迁移方向显著影响结果，且同一源-目标对在不同翻译目标下表现差异明显；拉丁化在脚本不匹配情况下普遍提升BLEU和chrF，但不同指标间效果不一致；迁移源在多种设置下保持稳定。

Conclusion: 跨语言迁移在突厥语族中受语言亲缘关系和迁移方向显著影响，且迁移源具有高度稳定性。拉丁化可有效缓解脚本不匹配问题，但其收益依赖于评估指标。该研究为低资源突厥语翻译提供了重要实证支持。

Abstract: Cross-lingual transfer is central to low-resource machine translation, but its behavior within closely related language families remains insufficiently characterized. We study transfer among five Turkic languages; Turkish, Azerbaijani, Uzbek, Kazakh, and Kyrgyz; using pairwise transfer matrices. In this setting, each model is fine-tuned with one transfer source and evaluated on a different transfer target while the translation target remains the same. Across mT5 experiments, we find that transfer is strongest between closely related Turkic pairs, especially Turkish-Azerbaijani and Kazakh-Kyrgyz. We also show that transfer direction matters, and that the same transfer source-transfer target pair can behave differently when the translation target changes. Latinization improves BLEU and chrF in several script-mismatched settings, but its effect is not uniform across metrics. Additional analyses show that transfer sources are mostly stable across different datasets and model settings.

</details>


### [116] [Zero-Mem: Zero-Token Memory Operations for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29377)
*Yilin Xiao,Zhehan Zhu,Yujing Zhang,Jin Chen,Zijin Hong,Luyao Zhuang,Qinggang Zhang,Shengyuan Chen,Xiaocao Ouyang,Lingfei Ren,Xiao Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Zero-Mem提出一种无需生成中间表示的零令牌记忆操作方法，通过构建实体-上下文图和时间层次结构来组织交互记录，仅在最终问答时调用一次LLM，显著降低内存操作的时间和令牌开销，在长记忆和长上下文问答任务中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理的记忆系统依赖额外的LLM调用来管理记忆，导致重复的令牌和时间成本，且容易丢失或合并关键信息；本文旨在探索是否可以通过不生成中间表示的方式实现结构化记忆访问。

Method: Zero-Mem将原始交互轨迹作为记忆源，构建实体-上下文图以揭示跨交互连接，同时建立时间层次结构以保留会话局部性和状态；针对每个查询，融合两种视图进行检索，并通过确定性校准去除冲突证据，确保答案基于原始记录。

Result: 在多个长记忆与长上下文问答基准上，Zero-Mem达到竞争性性能，完全消除记忆操作中的LLM调用和令牌消耗，相比最快基线减少57.6%的内存操作时间成本，消融实验验证了两种记忆结构及其动态协调的有效性。

Conclusion: 结构化代理记忆无需生成中间表示，可实现高效、低成本且保持原始证据完整性的记忆管理。

Abstract: LLM agents need memory to act consistently over long interactions, yet many systems use additional LLM calls to operate that memory. Generating intermediate records and mediating their retrieval adds recurring token and time costs, while omitted or merged details can obscure the original evidence. We ask whether structured memory access requires generation at all. Zero-Mem introduces \emph{zero-token memory operations}: no step outside final question answering invokes an LLM or consumes LLM input or output tokens; encoder computation is accounted for separately. Zero-Mem preserves original interaction traces as its source of record. It organizes the traces in two complementary ways. An entity--context graph exposes connections across interactions, while a temporal hierarchy preserves conversational locality and session state. For each query, Zero-Mem weighs the two views, retrieves from both, and follows their structure to recover supporting relations or surrounding context. Deterministic calibration first discards conflicting evidence and then keeps the reader's answer grounded in the retrieved traces. Only the final-QA reader invokes an LLM. Across long-memory and long-context question-answering benchmarks, Zero-Mem achieves competitive performance while eliminating LLM calls and LLM-token consumption from memory operations. With the same final-QA reader and context budget, it reduces memory-operation time cost by 57.6\% relative to the fastest compared baseline. Ablations support the contribution of the two views and their query-dependent coordination. Overall, the results show that structured agent memory need not generate an intermediate representation of the past. After peer review, the code and implementation details will be available at \textcolor{blue}{https://github.com/TheMoon0815/Zero-mem}.

</details>


### [117] [PTP: Previous-Token Prediction based LLM Inversion for Near-Exact Prompt Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29378)
*Pirzada Suhail,Nagasai Saketh Naidu,Atanu R Sinha,Amit Sethi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需访问模型权重或logits的黑箱逆向方法，通过在目标LLM自生成的数据上从零训练显式逆语言模型，利用前向next-token预测的对偶机制进行previous-token预测，实现高质量的提示重建。该方法支持多样化的提示重构，并在多个评估指标上优于现有工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统提示恢复方法依赖于预训练序列到序列模型的微调和外部数据集，且需要访问模型权重或logits，限制了其在黑箱场景下的应用。本文旨在解决这一问题，提出一种无需辅助资源、适用于黑箱环境的高效提示逆向方法。

Method: 提出一种基于前向-逆向生成链路的显式逆语言模型，通过在目标LLM自身生成的合成数据上训练，采用previous-token预测方式模拟逆过程，建立与前向生成的对偶关系，实现提示重建。

Result: 在多种数据集和不同LLM的响应上均表现出良好的泛化能力与可迁移性；在基于token的提示与响应重建评估中，性能优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 该方法为黑箱环境下大语言模型的提示逆向提供了有效解决方案，具备高精度、多样性与跨模型适应性，具有广泛应用潜力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) generate text by auto-regressively sampling the next token. This inherently leads to a many-to-many mapping between prompts and responses, complicating the task of inferring prompts from observed outputs. Prior work on LLM inversion frames prompt recovery as a semantic reconstruction task. They rely on fine-tuning pretrained sequence-to-sequence models on large external datasets--and requiring access to model weights or logits--to generate semantically plausible prompts. In contrast, we present a functional approach to inverting a given LLM in a black-box setting, without auxiliary aids. We train an explicit inverse language model entirely from scratch on data synthetically generated from the target LLM itself. Analogous to forward next-token prediction, our inverse model is trained using previous-token prediction, establishing a generative link between the forward and inverse processes that enables faithful prompt reconstruction. Moreover, it naturally supports diverse prompt reconstructions through sampling, whereby all such prompts induce similar responses under the forward, target LLM. Our approach generalises across datasets and exhibits transferability in reconstructing prompts from responses generated by different LLMs. Further, across the set of token based evaluation metrics for prompt and response reconstructions, our approach outperforms prior work.

</details>


### [118] [Studying quantization trade-offs for efficient inference deployment in machine translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29397)
*Jim Zhao,Sohir Maskey,Koen Oostermeijer,Douglas Orr,Teryn Jones*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了在单个A100或H100 GPU上部署大型语言模型时，量化技术对延迟和吞吐量的影响，重点关注EuroLLM和Hy-MT2两个翻译模型家族（1.7B至22B参数规模）。通过引入文档分块策略与W4A8/W8A8量化结合，显著优化了延迟-吞吐量权衡。同时，针对传统机器翻译评估仅基于孤立句子、无法反映长文本上下文的问题，提出WMT24++文档级评估，揭示了量化与长文档翻译质量间的复杂交互。结果显示，Hy-MT2对量化具有鲁棒性，而EuroLLM则表现出强烈敏感性，量化后翻译质量急剧下降。结论表明，推理效率与翻译质量的权衡不仅取决于量化格式，还受文本分块策略影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量化研究多关注内存占用与推理效率，但缺乏在真实服务器负载下对延迟与吞吐量的系统性评估；同时，标准机器翻译评测基于短句片段，无法反映长文档上下文下的量化影响，导致评估结果不可靠。因此亟需在可控工作负载下评估量化对长文本翻译性能的真实影响。

Method: 在单个A100/H100 GPU环境下，对5个不同规模的翻译模型（1.7B–22B）进行系统性实验，采用W4A8与W8A8量化，并结合文档分块策略。通过构建控制变量的工作负载场景，测量延迟与吞吐量表现；引入WMT24++文档级评估框架，对比量化前后长文档翻译质量变化，分析量化与分块策略的协同效应。

Result: 结合文档分块与W4A8/W8A8量化可显著改善延迟-吞吐量权衡曲线，在多种工作负载下提升系统性能。文档级评估显示，标准段落级评测无法预测量化对长文档翻译的影响：Hy-MT2在量化下仍保持稳定，而EuroLLM在所有量化格式下均出现翻译质量快速下降。量化策略与分块方式的组合对最终效果有决定性影响。

Conclusion: 量化带来的效率提升并非无代价，其对翻译质量的影响高度依赖模型架构与文本处理策略。为实现高效且高质量的部署，必须在量化方案设计中综合考虑模型特性与实际应用中的长文本处理需求，尤其是文档分块策略的选择至关重要。

Abstract: Deploying large language models in realistic server environments poses challenges, as the system needs to provide high-quality responses with low latency. Quantization is a common approach to reduce the memory footprint and improve inference efficiency, yet its impact on latency and throughput is rarely evaluated under controlled, orchestration-level workloads. In this work we study the quantization trade-offs of two translation model families, EuroLLM \citep{martins2025eurollm} and Hy-MT2 \citep{zheng2026hy} across five models ranging from 1.7B to 22B for efficient deployment on a single A100 or H100 GPU. We demonstrate that combining a document-chunking strategy with W4A8 or W8A8 quantization improves the latency-throughput Pareto-curve under a wide range of workloads. Furthermore, since standard machine translation (MT) benchmarks rely on isolated sentences and fail to capture long-context dynamics, we introduce a document-level evaluation from WMT24++ to assess how text chunking strategies affect translation quality under quantization. Our results reveal that standard segment-level evaluation can fail to predict the interaction between quantization and long-context document translation. While Hy-MT2 remains robust under quantization, EuroLLM shows strong sensitivity and translation quality collapses rapidly for all considered quantization formats. Overall, our experiments show that the trade-off between inference efficiency and translation quality depends not only on the quantization format, but also on the choice of text chunking strategy.

</details>


### [119] [Know It, Act on It: Investigating Memory Utilization in LLM Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29433)
*Zhaoxin Feng,Jianfei Ma,Emmanuele Chersoni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了大型语言模型（LLM）代理在个性化陪伴中面临的知识利用问题，即模型虽能记住用户偏好，却无法有效应用这些信息。通过引入解耦评估范式，对同一用户偏好进行‘知道’与‘行动’测试，发现多数情况下模型能回忆偏好但无法在行为中体现。尽管不同记忆架构有所改善，但在健康和心理治疗类偏好上表现仍差，影响重大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型作为个性化助手，需有效利用用户记忆以提供定制化服务，但现有模型常出现‘记得但不使用’的问题，导致服务失效。为理解这一现象，需要区分记忆失败与使用失败。

Method: 提出解耦评估框架，设计成对的‘知道’与‘行动’测试，针对16个系统和5种记忆架构，在三种表达强度下评估1000个用户偏好，量化模型的记忆与行为一致性。

Result: 结果显示，‘知道’与‘行动’之间存在显著差距；模型普遍能回忆偏好但难以在实际响应中体现；尤其在健康和心理治疗相关偏好上，行为应用能力最弱。

Conclusion: 当前大模型在记忆利用方面存在严重短板，即使具备记忆能力，也无法有效转化为行为响应。未来需重点优化知识利用机制，尤其是在高风险领域。

Abstract: As large language model (LLM) agents evolve into personalized companions, memory has emerged as a core capability. However, LLMs face a knowledge utilization problem: they may fail to act on relevant user preferences even when they are fully present in context. When an agent fails to tailor its response in a context where previously shared user preferences should matter, it is unclear whether the model failed to remember that information or remembered it but failed to use it. To isolate this breakdown, we introduce a decoupled evaluation paradigm that administers paired Know and Act tests to the same user preference. We conduct large-scale experiments across 16 systems and five memory architectures, evaluating 1,000 preferences embedded at three levels of expression strength. Our results show a large gap between Know and Act outcomes: agents often pass the recall test for a user preference but fail to reflect that same preference in the paired behavioral scenario. While memory architectures reduce this gap, utilization remains especially weak for health and therapy-related preferences, where failures to act carry the greatest real-world stakes.

</details>


### [120] [Evidence-Type Competition: When Can Interventional Data Teach Language Models Causal Direction?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29484)
*Xining Xun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在合成环境中测试干预数据作为因果推理教学黄金标准的有效性，发现其在辛普森悖论场景中失效：即使增加干预样本比例，模型仍受观测上下文影响而出现符号错误。因果方向判断取决于推理时的上下文证据类型，而非训练混合比例。移除观测证据可显著提升因果推断能力，且该效应稳定、可定位至中间层的观测行。此外，提出一种基于证据平均的协议，可将符号错误率从26%降至9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验干预数据是否真正能有效引导模型学习因果推理，特别是在存在观测相关性与因果效应符号相反的复杂情境下。

Method: 在可控合成环境中构建辛普森悖论世界，对比观测与干预数据对模型因果判断的影响；通过改变训练混合比例和推理上下文，分析模型行为变化；使用激活修补（activation patching）定位关键神经元；评估不同采样策略下的误差率。

Result: 模型的因果响应符号由观测上下文主导，即使大量使用干预数据也无法纠正；去除观测信息后，因果推断准确率显著提升（41/50正确）；该抑制机制稳定存在于不同训练种子和参数规模下；外部审计揭示模型存在正向效应先验，可通过重训练消除分布内偏差但无法消除分布外偏差。

Conclusion: 模型的因果推理能力存在于权重中，但其能否被激活取决于推理时的上下文内容；上下文中的观测证据会抑制因果推断，该现象是内容驱动且可分级的；建议采用证据平均等策略降低符号错误。

Abstract: Interventional data is widely regarded as the gold standard for teaching models causal reasoning. We test this assumption in a fully controlled synthetic environment pitting observational correlation against causal effect, and find it fails instructively. In Simpson's-paradox worlds, where the two have systematically opposite signs, increasing the fraction of interventional samples in pretraining does not improve causal direction: the magnitude of the model's do()-response grows monotonically, yet its sign is copied from the observational context. What governs whether interventional evidence is used is not the training mixture but the evidence type present in the context at inference time. Under an identical training recipe, a purely observational context induces systematic sign reversal in 29/50 worlds, a mixed context in 19/50, while aligned interventional probes alone yield 41/50 correct. Erasing observational evidence from the context immediately releases the suppressed causal interpolation ability (ratio_true = +0.56); a four-state content manipulation shows the switch is content-mediated and graded. The suppression is stable across training seeds (11/11 strong reversals persist on a matched-protocol second seed) and robust as a rate at 0.93B parameters (31.8% vs. 6% reversals in the matched probe-only arm), even as absolute gains shrink four-fold. An external audit on CLadder exposes a learned positive-effect prior with a two-layer structure: sign-randomized retraining removes it in-distribution but not out-of-distribution. We summarize: the capability lives in the weights; the switch lives in the context, and activation patching localizes the switch to the middle layers' observational rows. We further quantify the sampling noise floor of probe-based causal evaluation and an evidence-averaging protocol that cuts sign errors from 26% to 9%.

</details>


### [121] [ARB: A Matched Authorship-Rewriting Benchmark Dataset for AI-Text Detector Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29539)
*Gaetano Perrone,Simon Pietro Romano*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出作者重写基准（ARB），评估AI文本检测器在人类文本经LLM重写后的表现。结果表明，传统检测器在直接生成的LLM文本上表现良好，但在人类文本被LLM重写后性能大幅下降（如FastDetectGPT从91.2%降至30.8%），而对纯LLM文本重写的鲁棒性则较强。这说明现有检测器在常规评测中表现不能反映其在真实场景中的实际效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI文本检测基准主要比较人类写作与直接由大语言模型生成的内容，但未涵盖人类内容经LLM重写的真实场景。由于重写会显著影响检测器性能，因此需要新的评估基准来衡量检测器在更贴近实际应用情境下的有效性。

Method: 构建了包含1,800篇人类原文（来自XSum、WritingPrompts、OpenWebText）和四种开源模型（Llama-3.2-3B、Qwen2.5-7B、Mistral-7B、Gemma-2-9B）的作者重写基准（ARB）。每篇原文生成四类变体：人类原文（HUMAN）、直接LLM生成（Free-LLM）、LLM重写的原文（H2L）、同一模型重写的LLM文本（LLM2L），并使用五种检测器在严格1%假阳性率下进行评估。

Result: FastDetectGPT和Binoculars-falcon-7b在直接生成文本上检测率达91.2%和93.5%，但在人类文本被重写后分别降至30.8%和15.1%，下降60-78个百分点；而在纯LLM文本重写时仅下降10-13个百分点。RADAR也呈现类似趋势（66.8%降至12.2%），而BERT-Defense和RoBERTa-Defense在所有情况下均低于3%。

Conclusion: 当前基于人类与直接生成文本对比的检测器评测无法准确预测其在人类内容经LLM重写场景下的表现。检测器对人类文本重写具有显著脆弱性，即使对纯LLM文本重写仍保持一定鲁棒性。因此，需建立更贴近现实的应用评估标准。

Abstract: Standard AI-text detection benchmarks compare human-written text against text generated directly by large language models (LLMs). While prior work has shown that rewriting and paraphrasing can degrade detector performance, it remains unclear whether performance measured on this conventional benchmark predicts detector behavior when human-authored content is rewritten by an LLM. To address this gap, we introduce Authorship-Rewriting Benchmark (ARB), built from 1,800 human source texts (600 each from XSum, WritingPrompts, and OpenWebText) and four open-weight generators (Llama-3.2-3B, Qwen2.5-7B, Mistral-7B, Gemma-2-9B). Each source item yields four matched variants: human-written (HUMAN), direct LLM generation (Free-LLM), LLM-rewritten human text (H2L), and same-generator LLM-rewritten LLM text (LLM2L). We evaluated five detectors (FastDetectGPT, Binoculars-falcon-7b, RADAR, BERT-Defense, RoBERTa-Defense) at a strict 1%-false-positive operating point (TPR@1%FPR). FastDetectGPT and Binoculars-falcon-7b detected 91.2% and 93.5\% of direct LLM text, but only 30.8% and 15.1% of human text an LLM had rewritten, a drop of 60-78 percentage points. The same detectors retained 78.3% and 83.0% recall when LLM text was rewritten by the same model, a much smaller decline of 10-13 points. RADAR followed the same pattern (66.8% to 12.2%), while BERT-Defense and RoBERTa-Defense stayed below 3% recall across all regimes. These results show that detector performance measured on the conventional human-vs-LLM benchmark does not transfer to human-authored text revised by an LLM, even though the same detectors remain largely robust to LLM-only rewriting.

</details>


### [122] [Sycophancy Undermines Epistemic Vigilance in Cooperative Vision-Language Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29585)
*Rupak Sarkar,Neha Srikanth,Saloni Gupta,Claire Bonial,Philip Resnik,Rachel Rudinger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于对话的“找不同”任务，用于衡量视觉-语言模型在信息不对称合作场景中的认知警觉性。研究发现，模型常因过度迎合对话伙伴而忽略自身私有证据，表现出类似‘奉承’的行为。通过使用任务无关的奉承样本学习向量来引导模型，可有效减少此类错误，提升模型对自身证据的忠实报告能力，从而增强其在复杂协作任务中的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统需具备在合作任务中检测信息冲突并修复矛盾的能力，以维持共同认知基础；当前模型在信息不对称对话中常忽视自身证据，盲目顺从对话伙伴，影响协作可靠性。

Method: 设计一个信息不对称的对话式‘找不同’任务，两个模型各自观察一张图片，通过对话判断图片是否相同或找出差异；通过分析模型行为，识别其是否因过度迎合而忽略私有信息，并采用从非任务相关奉承样本中学习的向量进行模型引导以减轻该问题。

Result: 模型普遍表现出对私有证据的忽视，倾向于与对话伙伴保持一致，即使该一致性是错误的；使用学习到的引导向量可显著降低此类错误，提升模型对自身证据的忠实度和协作可靠性。

Conclusion: 模型的认知警觉性不足是其在信息不对称协作任务中表现不佳的关键原因；通过模型引导减少奉承行为，能有效提升其对私有证据的重视程度，使其成为更可靠的协作伙伴。

Abstract: To maintain common ground in cooperative conversation, humans iteratively update their beliefs as conversation participants share new information; participants who are epistemically vigilant detect when new information conflicts with prior beliefs and take steps to repair these conflicts. In order for AI systems to serve as reliable partners in complex cooperative tasks, they must similarly weigh incoming information against their own private evidence and shared context and appropriately surface inconsistencies when they arise. To measure the epistemic vigilance of vision-language models in cooperative settings, we present an information-asymmetric, dialog-based "spot-the-difference" task. Two models are privately shown one image each, and must determine through conversation whether the images are identical or, if not, identify the difference. Models routinely fail at this: they frequently overlook key evidence in their private image in favor of agreeing with their conversational partner, even when their agreement is unwarranted. We relate these violations of epistemic vigilance to the broader behavior of sycophancy, which manifests itself in cooperative goal-oriented dialog as over-accommodation and weak evidential grounding. Our results show that model steering to reduce sycophancy with a vector learned from task-agnostic sycophancy examples can reduce epistemic vigilance-related errors, making models more faithful reporters of their evidence, and in turn, more reliable partners in information-asymmetric cooperative tasks.

</details>


### [123] [ResKV: Reconstructing Omitted Attention Contributions for Fixed-Budget KV Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29591)
*Yuhang Zhan,Lisi Chen,Shuo Shang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ResKV提出一种新的KV缓存压缩方法，通过将固定缓存预算分为精确主缓存和紧凑残差缓存，重建被丢弃令牌的注意力贡献。残差项在softmax归一化中与主缓存项共同参与，恢复注意力分子和分母的质量，而非作为事后修正。通过构建时验证代理和解码时动态门控机制，实现高效且准确的长上下文推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有缓存淘汰方法永久丢弃未选中的令牌，导致其注意力贡献丢失；基于合并的方法虽保留更多信息但可能扰动保留的键值。因此需要一种既能保持信息完整性又不破坏原有键值精确性的新方法。

Method: ResKV将缓存分为精确主缓存和紧凑残差缓存，利用残差统计重构被忽略令牌的注意力贡献，并在softmax中统一归一化，使残差项同时恢复分子与分母质量。采用构建时验证代理分配残差，解码时动态门控调节残差贡献。

Result: 在LongBench和RULER上的全面评估显示，ResKV在相同保留KV预算下显著优于现有压缩基线，同时保持压缩解码的实际效率，包括峰值内存占用和长上下文解码吞吐量。

Conclusion: ResKV通过合理分离主缓存与残差信息，在不牺牲效率的前提下有效恢复被舍弃的注意力贡献，为长上下文推理提供了更优的缓存压缩方案。

Abstract: KV cache compression is essential for efficient long-context inference. Existing eviction methods permanently discard unselected tokens and consequently remove their aggregate contribution to attention. Merging-based alternatives preserve more information but can perturb retained keys and values that should remain exact. We observe that the information omitted by cache eviction can be formulated as residual statistics in both the numerator and denominator of softmax attention. Based on this observation, we propose ResKV, which divides a fixed KV budget into an exact main cache and a compact residual cache that reconstructs the contribution of omitted tokens. ResKV lets main-cache tokens and residual entries participate in the same softmax normalization, so residual entries restore both attention numerator and denominator mass rather than acting as a post-hoc correction. A construction-time validation proxy determines residual allocation for each layer and KV head, while a decode-time dynamic gate adjusts residual contributions for individual queries. Comprehensive evaluations on LongBench and RULER, covering query-aware and query-agnostic settings, multiple backbones, cache budgets, and representative compression baselines, demonstrate broad improvements under the same retained KV budget while preserving the practical efficiency of compressed decoding, including peak memory usage and long-context decode throughput.

</details>


### [124] [FriendBench: Benchmarking Dyadic Familiarity Inference in Humans and Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29602)
*Jeffrey M. Girard,Jason Z. Zheng,Jacqueline R. Vertino,Antony D'Avirro,Benjamin Peloquin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FriendBench是一个用于判断两人是熟悉还是初次见面的基准测试，基于20秒的双人破冰对话片段。通过文本、音频和视频多模态数据，对比26个来自七家公司的模型与人类小组的表现。最佳模型与人类在准确率上无统计差异，但人类保持平衡判断，而模型倾向于“陌生人”，体现不同先验。视觉信息对人类更有帮助，而模型受益不均。数据与结果已公开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解社交情境依赖行为而非仅语言，需要一个能评估模型在人际互动中识别熟悉度能力的基准。

Method: 构建包含96个平衡配对的20秒双人对话片段，使用文本、音频、视频多模态输入，评估26个模型与人类小组在识别熟悉度上的表现。

Result: 最佳模型与人类在各模态下的准确率无显著差异，但模型更倾向判断为‘陌生人’，且视觉信息对人类提升更大。

Conclusion: 模型与人类在识别人际熟悉度上表现相当，但机制不同；视觉信息对人类更有效，未来研究需关注模型的先验偏差与多模态融合策略。

Abstract: Reading a social situation often depends on behavior, not words alone. We introduce FriendBench, a benchmark for inferring whether two people are already familiar or are meeting as strangers, from a 20-second clip of a dyadic ice-breaker conversation. Every pair answers the same type of prompt, so only the manner of interaction can reveal the answer. Across text, audio, and video, we compare 26 models from seven companies against matched human panels over 96 balanced dyads. The best model and the human crowd are statistically indistinguishable on accuracy in every modality, but reach it differently: humans stay balanced across the two answers, while the strongest models lean toward "stranger"---a difference in effective prior, not discrimination. Richer channels help both unequally, and only humans gain from visible behavior on top of speech. We release the stimuli, human ratings, and model predictions.

</details>


### [125] [Evolving language compositionality in a frequency-structured meaning space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29642)
*Fabio De Ponte,Eloise Gaines-White,Conor Houghton,Seth Bullock*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究频率分布对语言组合性演化的影响，发现高频意义可脱离低频意义的语法约束，但若频率分布作用于更小的语言单位而非整体语义单元，则语言无法跨代传递。这表明频率仅在可整体习得的形式-意义单位上才能塑造语言结构，否则会破坏组合泛化所需的关联结构，导致语言传播失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究频率分布如何影响语言组合性的形成，特别是自然语言中高频意义为何能突破语法约束，以及频率分布方式对语言稳定传递的影响。

Method: 基于迭代学习模型，模拟语言在多代传递中的演化过程，对比不同频率分布模式（整体语义向量 vs. 部分元素）对语言结构和传承稳定性的影响。

Result: 当频率分布作用于整体意义时，高频意义可摆脱语法约束，体现自然语言特征；而当频率分布作用于部分元素时，尽管高频成分被可靠习得，语言仍无法跨代传递，因缺乏支持组合泛化的结构性关系。

Conclusion: 频率对语言结构的塑造仅在频率分布作用于可整体习得的形式-意义单元时有效；若频率分布于细小单元，则破坏组合性基础，阻碍语言稳定传承。

Abstract: The iterated learning model was introduced to investigate language evolution: the way in which the characteristic properties of human languages have been shaped, at least partly, by repeated transmission from one language user to another. The key finding is that language compositionality can arise spontaneously as a consequence of language being passed repeatedly through a language learning bottleneck. Here we explore how changing the frequency of different meanings, so that some meanings occur much more frequently than others, affects the character of its compositionality. We find that, as observed in natural languages, high-frequency meanings can escape the pressure to conform to the grammar that characterizes lower-frequency meanings. However, when the frequency structure is instead imposed on parts rather than on whole meaning vectors, the language fails to transmit across generations. This occurs despite the fact that the most frequent elements are reliably learned. These results suggest that frequency can shape emergent linguistic structure only when the frequency distribution is defined over form-meaning units that learners can acquire holistically. When frequency is instead distributed over smaller units, it fails to support the relational structure required for compositional generalisation, thereby preventing stable language transmission.

</details>


### [126] [TokTier: Exact Stateful Tokenization for Agentic LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29678)
*Zhenyu Zhang,Zhichao Cao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TokTier 是一种状态化分词服务，通过增量重分词与稳定边界检查，在保持分词结果一致性的同时显著降低延迟。它在 17 种分词器上实现零分歧，相比传统方法提速最高达 491 倍，支持每秒处理 1,821 个请求，显著提升大模型服务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 服务系统虽缓存提示的 KV 状态，但前端仍每次重新对完整请求文本进行分词，导致编码代理在每次小工具返回后重复处理长文本，且因微小追加改变分词边界，难以复用。这使得分词耗时高达首次响应时间的 64%，成为性能瓶颈。

Method: TokTier 采用状态化分词策略：对会话续接仅重分词追加部分的小窗口，并通过稳定边界检查决定是否拼接；失败则回退至全量分词。对无可复用前缀的请求，将 GPT 家族正则预分词分解为本地规则，结合 GPU 执行精确预分词与 BPE 编码。通过采样影子验证器实时校验流量。

Result: 在 1.5×10^10 次分词比对、12.4TB 实际文本和 9.3 万次代理步骤中，未发现任何分词差异。增量修复耗时 0.5–1.1ms（100K 到 3M 字符），最快比 Hugging Face 分词快 437 倍，比最强缓存基线（Gigatoken）快 2.1 倍。GPU 全量分词处理 100 万字符仅需 0.87ms，比 HF 快 491 倍，比最快 CPU 方法快 23.4 倍。使用 vLLM 时，中位首次响应时间下降 16–34%，P99 下降 23%。在 50ms P99 目标下，四核修复 + 一 GPU 可维持 1,821 请求/秒，远超 16 核无状态前端的 40 请求/秒。

Conclusion: TokTier 证明了状态化、增量式分词在大规模语言模型服务中的可行性与高效性，实现了极低延迟与高吞吐，同时保证分词一致性，是提升大模型服务性能的关键优化。

Abstract: LLM serving systems cache prompt KV state, yet most front ends still re-tokenize the full request text on every call. The cost lands on coding agents, which resubmit a long transcript after each small tool result, and reuse is hard because even a short append can change token boundaries near the end of the previous sequence. Across 153,951 calls from two agent ecosystems, the median call appends about 1.4K characters, and only 1.0-3.6% of calls start or rebuild a session with contexts of millions of characters. At a 94.1% fleet prompt-cache hit rate, tokenization reaches up to 64% of time to first token.
  TokTier is a stateful tokenization service with one contract: emitted token IDs are always identical to full reference tokenization of the request text. For a session continuation, it re-tokenizes a small window around the append and splices only after a per-request stable-boundary check, widening the window or falling back to full tokenization on failure. For a call without a reusable prefix, it decomposes GPT-family regex pre-tokenization into run-local rules and runs exact pre-tokenization and BPE on a GPU. A sampled shadow verifier re-checks live traffic.
  Across 17 tokenizer families, differential campaigns cover 1.5x10^10 split checks, a 12.4 TB real-text corpus, and 93,000+ replayed agent steps, with zero divergence. Incremental repair takes 0.5-1.1 ms from 100K to 3M characters, up to 437x faster than HF tokenization and 2.1x faster at 1M than the strongest cache-based baseline (Gigatoken) fully prewarmed. GPU full tokenization encodes a 1M-character request in 0.87 ms, up to 491x below HF and 23.4x below the fastest published CPU method. With vLLM, median time to first token drops 16-34% and P99 drops 23% under recorded bursts. Under a 50 ms P99 objective, four repair cores plus one GPU sustain 1,821 requests/s where a 16-core stateless front end saturates at 40.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [127] [OpenClaw and Ollama in Agentic AI: Toward Fully Autonomous and Scalable AI Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28629)
*Konstantinos I. Roumeliotis,Ranjan Sapkota*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种全面的分层架构，用于构建从被动大语言模型到具有记忆、规划和持续执行能力的自主智能体的转变。通过分析 OpenClaw 与 Ollama 的集成系统，验证了系统级整合在实现持久记忆、工具使用和自适应决策中的关键作用，并强调了架构复杂性提升带来的性能改善。研究还探讨了可扩展性、安全、隐私、治理和评估等挑战，提出未来需发展多智能体、分布式自治系统及以人为本的智能体框架。所有模型、代码和数据集均已公开，以支持复现与基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前从反应式大语言模型向持久、可行动的智能体系统过渡暴露出对智能体架构理解的不足，尤其在推理、编排与执行层分离方面存在明显空白。现有统一框架在设计与评估全栈智能体系统方面仍不完善，亟需建立系统化的架构基础与评估方法。

Method: 提出一种分层架构，将智能体系统划分为推理（如Ollama）、编排（如OpenClaw）与执行层；通过构建OpenClaw-Ollama全栈系统原型，进行实验验证；采用系统集成方式评估持久记忆、工具调用与自适应决策能力的涌现。

Result: 系统级集成显著提升了智能体的持久记忆、工具利用与自适应决策能力；随着架构复杂度增加，系统性能持续提升；验证了全栈设计的有效性，同时揭示了在可扩展性、安全、隐私与评估方面的关键挑战。

Conclusion: 本研究建立了统一的智能体架构基础，证明了全栈自主智能体系统的有效性，并为构建可扩展、安全、可信的智能体系统提供了路线图。所有资源公开，推动领域标准化与可复现性。

Abstract: The rapid transition from reactive large language models (LLMs) to persistent, action-capable systems has exposed critical gaps in the architectural understanding of Agentic AI, particularly in separating inference, orchestration, and execution layers for autonomous AI agents. Despite recent advances, unified frameworks for designing and evaluating full-stack agentic systems remain limited. This paper presents a comprehensive, layered architecture for Agentic AI, outlining the evolution from reactive LLM interfaces to persistent, goal-driven autonomous AI agents with memory, planning, and continuous execution. We analyze OpenClaw and Ollama as a full-stack Agentic AI system, where Ollama serves as the LLM inference layer and OpenClaw enables agent runtime orchestration, integrating reasoning, tool use, and action execution. A prototype experimental validation of the OpenClaw-Ollama architecture demonstrates that capabilities such as persistent memory, tool utilization, and adaptive decision-making emerge from system-level integration rather than standalone models, with performance improving consistently as architectural complexity increases. The study further examines challenges in scalability, security, privacy, governance, and evaluation of agentic systems, highlighting the need for robust benchmarking and system-level design. Future directions include scalable multi-agent architectures, distributed autonomous systems, and human-aware Agentic AI frameworks for responsible deployment. Overall, this work establishes a unified architectural foundation for Agentic AI, validates the effectiveness of full-stack autonomous AI agents, and provides a roadmap for building scalable, secure, and trustworthy agentic systems. All models, code, and datasets are publicly released to support reproducibility and benchmarking.

</details>


### [128] [Can AI Evaluate AI Scientists? A Benchmarking Study of Autonomous Research Generation Systems Using Automated Multi-Model Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28631)
*Vaibhava Lakshmi Ravideshik,Mayank Kejriwal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于前沿大语言模型的自动化同行评审系统，用于评估AI科学家系统的科研论文质量。通过在四个核心维度（原创性、科学严谨性、清晰度和重要性）上对四个主流AI科学家框架（Sakana AI v1/v2、CycleResearcher、Data-to-Paper）及FARS基准论文进行评估，发现FARS生成的论文显著优于其他系统，其平均得分（2.14–2.47）远高于其他系统（1.00–1.87），且在多数评价中超过两倍。Gemini与Claude评分一致性高（ρ=0.907），且与综合评分高度相关（ρ=0.961），验证了评估体系的可靠性；而GPT-5.4表现较差，表明其评价标准不一致。该研究建立了首个可量化的AI科学家系统基准，证明多模型LLM评估是可扩展、一致的自主科研质量评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏对AI科学家系统生成科研论文质量的可靠评估方法，亟需建立一个客观、可重复的基准测试体系以推动该领域发展。

Method: 采用自动化同行评审系统，利用GPT-5.4、Gemini和Claude三个独立的大语言模型作为评审者，从原创性、科学严谨性、清晰度和重要性四个维度对60篇由不同AI框架生成的论文及15篇FARS基准论文进行评分，并分析评分一致性与相关性。

Result: FARS基准论文在四项指标上均显著优于其他系统，平均得分2.14–2.47，为所有系统中最高；Gemini与Claude评分高度一致且与综合评分强相关，而GPT-5.4评分一致性较低，显示其评价标准存在差异。

Conclusion: 本研究首次建立了量化评估AI科学家系统的基准，证实多模型大语言模型评估具有可扩展性和一致性，是衡量自主科研质量的有效工具。

Abstract: AI Scientist systems capable of autonomous research have the potential to significantly accelerate scientific discovery. However, evaluating and comparing the quality of AI-generated papers remains an open challenge. We propose and implement a rigorous benchmarking protocol using an automated peer-review system that harnesses frontier large language models to assess scientific papers across four core dimensions: originality, scientific rigor, clarity, and significance. We evaluate four leading AI Scientist frameworks: \textit{Sakana AI (v1 & v2)}, \textit{CycleResearcher}, and \textit{Data-to-Paper}. Each framework was run on a consistent set of 15 research proposals published by a commercial autonomous AI scientist company (FARS), generating 60 papers that we evaluate alongside 15 FARS benchmark papers. Using three independent LLM reviewers (GPT-5.4, Gemini, and Claude), we find that FARS benchmark papers significantly outperform all competing frameworks, achieving mean scores of 2.14--2.47 on a 1--5 scale compared to 1.00--1.87 for other systems. Notably, FARS scores are more than 2$\times$ higher than the next-best systems on Gemini and Claude evaluations. We find strong agreement among Gemini and Claude ($ρ$ = 0.907, $p < 0.001$), and both correlate extremely strongly with the synthesis score ($ρ$ = 0.961, $p < 0.001$), validating the reliability of automated evaluation. However, GPT-5.4 exhibits weaker agreement ($ρ\approx 0.32$), suggesting it evaluates papers using different criteria. These results establish the first quantitative benchmark for AI Scientist systems and demonstrate that multi-model LLM evaluation provides a scalable, consistent framework for assessing autonomous research quality.

</details>


### [129] [LLM Framework for Discovering Major Mathematical Conjectures: AI's Quest for the Next Riemann Hypothesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28632)
*Alizer Wong,Zixin Zeng,Yi Tan,Wenyuan Li,Xuhang Chen,Xingru Lai,Yang Shi,Liangsi Lu,Yanhui Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个三阶段的猜想发现流水线，旨在系统性地生成具有重大数学潜力的猜想。该方法结合局部证据搜索、反思性验证（评估基础性、新颖性和潜在重要性）以及在Lean 4和Mathlib中的形式化验证，目标是发现具有高‘问题品味’的数学问题，即那些可能重构研究领域语言并持久助力人类数学研究的问题。实验在20个候选猜想上进行，结果表明从自然语言到形式化检查的转换稳定，所有20个候选均通过Lean解析与类型检查，未被exact?直接吸收，也未被aesop自动证明，且无重复或近似重复项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前重大数学猜想仍高度依赖专家直觉，缺乏统一的方法来系统生成和验证具有重大数学潜力的猜想，因此亟需一种可扩展、可验证的机制以提升猜想发现的效率与可靠性。

Method: 提出三阶段猜想发现流程：(1) 基于显式局部证据的区域搜索；(2) 反思性验证，评估猜想的基础性、新颖性及潜在重要性；(3) 在Lean 4与Mathlib中进行形式化验证，确保逻辑严谨性。

Result: 在20个候选猜想上的实验显示，所有候选均成功通过自然语言到形式化系统的转换，全部通过Lean的解析与类型检查；未被exact?直接吸收，也未被aesop自动证明，且无重复或近似重复项，表明该方法具备可行性与有效性。

Conclusion: 该三阶段方法为重大数学猜想的系统性发现提供了可行路径，能够有效生成高质量、具高问题品味的数学猜想，推动数学研究向更可验证、可复现的方向发展。

Abstract: Major mathematical conjectures still depend heavily on expert intuition, so a unified method for the systematic generation and validation of conjectures with substantial mathematical potential remains unavailable. We present a three stage pipeline for major conjecture discovery, with region search from explicit local evidence modules, reflective validation for foundationality, novelty, and potential significance, and formal validation in Lean 4 and Mathlib. The objective is the discovery of mathematical problems with high problem taste, namely problems whose proofs could reorganize the language of a research area and provide durable help to human mathematical research. Experiments on twenty candidates showstable passage from natural language to formal checks, with twenty out of twenty candidates passing Lean parsing and type checking, twenty out of twenty candidates not directly absorbed by exact?,twenty out of twenty candidates not automatically discharged by aesop, and no explicit duplicates or near duplicates.

</details>


### [130] [ThinkReset: Learnable Intermediate Interface Construction for Bounded-Context Long-Horizon Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28642)
*Fei Ding,Yongkang Zhang,Runhao Liu,Yuhao Liao,Zijian Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ThinkReset提出通过构建可重用的中间接口来解决长链推理中的冗余积累、上下文溢出和错误锚定问题，特别针对在有限上下文窗口下因最终答案奖励导致的过早猜测问题。该方法通过接口写回与重置机制，在多个长时序推理基准上显著提升固定上下文窗口下的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长链推理虽能提升复杂问题求解性能，但在有限上下文窗口下易产生冗余积累、上下文溢出和错误锚定；现有方法未解决核心瓶颈——缺乏可复用的中间接口来替代被丢弃的历史信息，且结局奖励驱动的强化学习易导致过早猜测。

Method: 提出ThinkReset，通过显式构建可重用的中间接口（接口写回与重置），直接优化重置后的持续求解成功率，以克服历史信息丢失和错误累积问题。

Result: 在多个长时序推理基准测试中，ThinkReset在固定上下文窗口下均显著提升任务成功率达到一致水平，验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: 核心瓶颈并非轨迹压缩或测试时控制，而是缺少可复用的中间接口；ThinkReset通过接口写回与重置机制，有效支持持续推理，是提升长链推理性能的关键路径。

Abstract: Long chain-of-thought reasoning improves performance on complex problems, but it also introduces redundancy accumulation, context overflow, and error anchoring. We argue that under bounded context windows, the core bottleneck is not trajectory compression or test-time control, but the absence of a reusable intermediate interface that can replace discarded history and support continued solving. We further identify a key failure mode of outcome-reward-driven long-chain reinforcement learning: when the model has not solved the task before the window is nearly exhausted, the final-answer reward encourages premature guessing rather than continued careful reasoning. We propose ThinkReset, a text-space instantiation of this view. ThinkReset explicitly constructs reusable intermediate interfaces through interface writeback and reset, and directly optimizes post-reset continuation success. Across multiple long-horizon reasoning benchmarks, this perspective consistently improves success rates under fixed context windows.

</details>


### [131] [TAPR: Enhancing LLM Performance with a Task-Aware Prompt Rewriter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28657)
*Oliver Savolainen,Emanuele Bastianelli,Hosein Azarbonyad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种任务感知的提示重写模型TAPR，通过强化学习与组相对策略优化（GRPO）训练，利用大语言模型作为评判者来评估重写后的提示和任务输出，从而提升下游LLM性能。实验表明，该方法在问答、摘要和算术推理等任务上均显著提升了提示重写效果，使微调后的Phi-4-mini-instruct生成更清晰、更具指导性的提示，进而提高在Natural Questions和GSM8K等基准上的准确率。代码已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型需要精心设计的提示才能发挥最佳性能，但这对非专家用户构成障碍。因此，亟需一种自动化的方法来优化提示，以降低使用门槛并提升模型表现。

Method: 提出Task-Aware Prompt Rewriter (TAPR)模型，采用强化学习与组相对策略优化（GRPO）进行训练，奖励信号来自LLM-as-judge对重写提示及其对应任务输出的评估。

Result: 在问答、摘要、算术推理等任务上，TAPR显著优于基线模型；微调后的Phi-4-mini-instruct生成的提示更具可读性和指导性，使下游任务在Natural Questions和GSM8K等基准上准确率提升。

Conclusion: TAPR能够有效将原始提示转化为任务优化的高质量提示，显著提升大语言模型的性能，且具备良好的泛化能力，为非专家用户提供了一种高效、自动化的提示优化方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) often require carefully crafted prompts to unlock their full potential, which can be a barrier for non-expert users. This work addresses the challenge by introducing a Task-Aware Prompt Rewriter (TAPR), a model that reformulates user prompts into task-optimized prompts with the explicit goal of improving downstream LLM performance. We train TAPR using reinforcement learning with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), where rewards are derived from LLM-as-judge evaluations of both the reformulated prompt and the corresponding task output. Experimental results on diverse tasks, such as question answering, summarization, and arithmetic reasoning, show that our method yields consistent gains over base models in prompt rewriting ability. Fine-tuning Phi-4-mini-instruct (as the base model for TAPR) produces prompts that contain clearer and more instructive language, leading to higher accuracy on established benchmarks such as Natural Questions and GSM8K. Our code is available at: https://github.com/OliverSavolainen/task-specific-prompt-rewriter

</details>


### [132] [Empowering Cross-Domain Sequential Recommendation with Hybrid Tokenization and Serial-Parallel Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28659)
*Yuxuan Hu,Yuhao Wang,Tianbo Huang,Chao Zhang,Ziwei Liu,Lihua Zhang,Xiangyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GenCDSR提出了一种高效且有效的跨域序列推荐生成框架，通过跨域混合标记化机制和多塔架构捕捉跨域共性和领域特异性，并采用串并行解码策略部分并行化生成过程，显著降低推理延迟。实验表明，相比现有最优方法，其平均准确率提升1.5%，推理延迟降低85.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成推荐方法在标记化阶段忽略跨域协同相关性，且使用如束搜索等低效解码策略，导致实时部署困难。

Method: 设计跨域混合标记化机制与多塔架构，利用分层共享-特定代码本联合建模跨域共性与领域差异；提出跨域串并行解码策略，基于分层语义标识结构实现部分并行生成，提升效率与一致性。

Result: 在三个公开数据集上，GenCDSR平均准确率提升1.5%，推理延迟降低85.1%，优于当前最先进基线方法。

Conclusion: GenCDSR通过创新的标记化与解码机制，在保持高精度的同时大幅降低推理延迟，为跨域序列推荐提供了高效可行的生成式解决方案。

Abstract: Cross-domain sequential recommendation (CDSR) aims to model users' dynamic interest transitions and sequential patterns across multiple domains. Recently, generative recommendation (GR) has emerged. It first learns semantic identifiers (SIDs) from item semantics and formulates recommendation as autoregressive generation. However, existing methods face two critical issues: (1) they ignore collaborative correlations across domains during tokenization, and (2) they adopt inefficient decoding strategies, such as beam search, during generation, which hinders real-time deployment. To address these limitations, we propose GenCDSR, an effective and efficient generative framework for CDSR. Specifically, we design a cross-domain hybrid tokenization mechanism with a multi-tower architecture to jointly capture cross-domain commonalities and domain-specific distinctions through hierarchical shared-specific and fine-grained codebooks. Furthermore, we develop a cross-domain serial-parallel decoding strategy that leverages the hierarchical SID structure to partially parallelize generation, significantly reducing inference latency while preserving generation consistency. Experiments on three public datasets show that GenCDSR achieves an average accuracy improvement of 1.5 percent and an average inference latency reduction of 85.1 percent compared with state-of-the-art baselines. The implementation code and datasets are available online: https://github.com/Applied-Machine-Learning-Lab/RecSys2026_GenCDSR.

</details>


### [133] [An Ontology-Guided, Deduplication-Aware Extraction Layer for Knowledge Graph Construction from Heterogeneous Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28662)
*Vaibhav Dangaich,Kevin Lewis,Kundeshwar Pundalik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种生产级的文档抽取层，通过结合实时嵌入相似性检索与微调的Qwen3.5-9B模型，实现对多格式文档流的高质量实体与关系抽取。系统采用双阶段提取与五阶段精炼流程，利用动态加载的本体片段减少94%的词汇表开销，并通过无模型推理的去重算法与嵌入冲突检测机制，显著提升知识图谱质量。在情报语料上，搜索召回率从70%提升至95%，且无误合并，修复了七类隐蔽错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在处理非结构化文档时存在实体类型不一致、名称变体、关系重复及同名个体混淆等问题，导致知识图谱质量不稳定，亟需一种可扩展、高精度、符合正式本体的自动化抽取系统。

Method: 系统接收Kafka中的文档元数据，按格式分发至对应处理器，使用本地部署的微调版Qwen3.5-9B模型进行两轮抽取；关键创新在于‘本体引导抽取’：通过嵌入相似性实时从图数据库中检索相关本体片段并注入提示词，大幅降低静态本体切片的维护成本；随后经过五阶段精炼：确定性清洗、跨块合并、第二轮关系抽取、六种无需模型推理的去重算法、以及基于嵌入的冲突解决子系统（冲突守卫确保无相似度可覆盖）。

Result: 在真实情报语料上的评估显示，系统将搜索召回率从约70%提升至95%，未引入任何误合并，成功纠正了包括文本截断、标题前缀重复等在内的七类隐蔽质量问题。

Conclusion: 本系统通过动态本体引导与多层次精炼机制，实现了高效、准确、可扩展的知识图谱构建，在保持低运维成本的同时显著提升了抽取质量，具备在真实生产环境中落地的能力。

Abstract: Large language models extract entities and relationships from unstructured documents fluently but inconsistently: type vocabularies fracture across documents, the same person surfaces under several name variants, relationships duplicate, and distinct individuals who share a name risk silent conflation. This paper presents the design, implementation, and empirical refinement of a production extraction layer that converts a live document stream into a validated knowledge graph aligned to a formal ontology. The system consumes document metadata from Kafka, routes PDF, spreadsheet, Office, and image content through handlers built for each format, and extracts entities and relationships in two passes using a locally hosted Qwen3.5-9B model tuned on the ontology. Its distinguishing component is ontology-guided extraction: the relevant slice of a curated ontology is retrieved live from a graph database by embedding similarity and injected into the extraction prompt, reducing catalog overhead by about 94 percent relative to static domain slices. Extracted results then pass through a refinement pipeline of five stages: deterministic cleaning, merging across chunks, a second pass for relationships, six deduplication algorithms that require no model inference, and an embedding resolution subsystem whose conflict guard no similarity score can override. Evaluation on intelligence corpora improved search recall from roughly 70 to 95 percent with no false merges, and corrected seven classes of silent quality defect, ranging from a bug that truncated source text by a single character to the systematic duplication of entities that carried title prefixes.

</details>


### [134] [How Hard Does It Think? Analyzing Step-Aware Reasoning Energy in LLM Chain-of-Thought Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28674)
*Hui Wei,Junda Wu,Sheldon Yu,Sizhe Zhou,Yizhu Jiao,Ming Zhong,Bowen Jin,Tong Yu,Shijia Pan,Jiawei Han,Julian McAuley*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SARE框架，通过CKA分析Transformer层间隐状态的核矩阵，量化链式思维中每一步的计算努力，揭示非均匀的能量分布和语义阶段转变，且内部几何动态包含超越输出信号的预测信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释性方法依赖输出层面信号或将处理深度简化为单一轨迹级标量，无法清晰反映单个推理步骤的计算努力分配。

Method: 使用中心核对齐（CKA）分析相邻Transformer层间令牌隐藏状态的格拉姆矩阵，捕捉跨令牌关系结构，并将推理轨迹建模为潜在语义状态间的转换。

Result: 在六个推理基准和三个开源大模型上，发现推理能量在不同步骤类型间高度不均，存在轨迹级指标无法察觉的相变；错误轨迹在关键推理节点处表现出系统性较低的能量；基于SARE的特征在多数情况下表现优于或等同于基于输出的置信度基线。

Conclusion: SARE能够以细粒度揭示推理过程中的计算努力分布，内部几何动态蕴含了丰富的预测信息，超越传统表面信号。

Abstract: Understanding how computational effort is allocated across individual chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning steps remains an open challenge: existing interpretability methods rely on output-level signals or collapse processing depth into a single trajectory-level scalar, leaving step-wise effort opaque. We propose Step-Aware Reasoning Energy (SARE), a geometric framework that quantifies effort at the granularity of individual CoT steps via Centered Kernel Alignment (CKA) between Gram matrices of token hidden states across adjacent transformer layers, capturing inter-token relational structure without requiring eigenvector alignment or cluster correspondence. SARE further contextualizes this energy within reasoning's semantic progression by modeling CoT trajectories as transitions among latent semantic states.
