We evaluate 12 widely used agents and conventional HPO baselines under a unified protocol. The results show that current agents exhibit measurable experimental optimization ability across domains, but still face clear limitations in sustained iterative refinement, complex log diagnosis, and consistent progress toward reported reference performance.

</details>


### [168] [Development of FDD-ON: an Ontology for VAV HVAC System Fault Detection and Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29657)
*Yimin Chen,Brian Fricke,Bo Shen,Jamie Lian,Mingkan Zhang,James Lo,Yun Zhang,Shi Ye,Jiajing Huang,Han Hu,Chujie Lu,Rui Tang,George Zhuang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为FDD-ON的模块化、可扩展的本体，用于形式化表示变风量（VAV）暖通空调（HVAC）系统中的组件、故障类型、症状状态、故障影响及相关属性。该本体整合了HVAC系统故障检测与诊断（FDD）语义，提供故障和症状属性的全面表示，并支持受控词汇表。FDD-ON还提供了全面的故障、症状和影响库，以捕捉VAV HVAC系统中各种运行异常及其后果。通过明确的因果关系链（原因-故障-症状-影响），FDD-ON为查询诊断知识、映射异构FDD输出以及开发互操作性FDD应用提供了机器可读的基础。基于公开的VAV HVAC系统数据集进行了评估，并在FDD开发应用中得到验证，结果表明FDD-ON为实现可扩展、透明且互操作的FDD解决方案奠定了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前故障检测与诊断（FDD）技术在建筑中的有效部署受限于缺乏结构化的领域知识，导致数据可解释性差、互操作性不足，形成信息孤岛，阻碍了数字孪生驱动的FDD框架和人工智能驱动的维护决策系统等应用的发展。因此，亟需一种统一的语义框架来整合异构数据源、多样化设备类型及多样的诊断输出。

Method: 提出FDD-ON——一种面向VAV HVAC系统的故障检测与诊断本体，通过形式化建模系统组件、故障类型、症状状态、故障影响及相关属性，构建受控词汇表，并建立故障-症状-影响之间的显式因果关系链，从而实现机器可读的诊断知识表达与集成。

Result: FDD-ON在公开的VAV HVAC系统数据集上进行了评估，并成功应用于FDD开发场景。实验结果表明，该本体能够有效支持跨系统、跨平台的诊断知识查询与映射，显著提升FDD解决方案的可扩展性、透明性和互操作性，为未来智能运维系统提供坚实语义基础。

Conclusion: FDD-ON作为一项基础性语义框架，为实现高效、透明、可互操作的故障检测与诊断系统提供了关键支撑，推动了智能建筑中数字化运维技术的发展。

Abstract: Fault detection and diagnosis (FDD) technology is essential for improving HVAC system reliability, energy efficiency, and maintenance effectiveness. However, effective deployment of FDD solutions in buildings requires structured domain knowledge that can bridge heterogeneous data sources, diverse equipment types, and varied diagnostic outputs. Limited data interpretability and interoperability within the FDD domain have led to fragmented information silos, hindering the implementation of FDD and related applications, such as the digital twin-enabled FDD frameworks and artificial intelligence (AI)-driven maintenance decision-making systems. This paper presents an FDD Ontology (FDD-ON), a modular and extensible ontology to formally represent variable air volume (VAV) HVAC system components, fault types, symptom statuses, fault impacts and associated attributes. FDD-ON integrates HVAC system FDD semantics to provide comprehensive representations of fault and symptom attributes, supported by the well-defined controlled vocabulary. Additionally, FDD-ON offers comprehensive fault, symptom, and impact libraries to capture a broad spectrum of operational abnormalities and their consequences in VAV HVAC systems. Through explicit contributing cause-fault-symptom-impact relations, FDD-ON serves as a machine-interpretable basis for querying diagnostic knowledge, mapping heterogeneous FDD outputs, and developing interoperable FDD-related applications. FDD-ON is evaluated using publicly available VAV HVAC system datasets and demonstrated through FDD development applications. Results indicate that FDD-ON provides a foundational semantic framework for advancing scalable, transparent, and interoperable FDD solutions across various applications.

</details>


### [169] [ExtractBench: A Benchmark for Schema-Guided Enterprise Document Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29677)
*Boyang Zhang,Adrian Lyjak,Eli Stewart,Zhaoqi Li,Simon Suo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ExtractBench 是首个综合评估值准确性、记录完整性、可追溯性及成本的架构引导提取基准，涵盖 4,869 页来自 370 份企业文档的多领域、多类型数据。通过独立系统一致性和人工验证构建高质量标注，采用无序值 F1 和词级/页级 F1 评估准确性和溯源能力。商业 VLM 在短文档上表现良好但长文档易截断，编码代理虽准确但成本高；LlamaExtract Agentic Plus 在三项指标中均领先，兼具高精度与低成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业工作流日益依赖智能体进行结构化信息提取，但现有评估缺乏对值准确性、完整性、可追溯性及成本的综合考量，亟需一个全面、可扩展的基准来推动该领域发展。

Method: 构建包含 370 份企业文档、8 个业务领域、67 种文档类型的 4,869 页数据集；采用独立系统一致性、已知值合成和人工验证相结合的方式进行标注；设计可扩展的标注与评估流程，支持大规模值准确率、记录完整性和源追溯性评估。

Result: 商业视觉语言模型在短文档上表现良好，但在长文档中常出现记录列表截断；编码代理保持高准确率但成本显著更高；LlamaExtract Agentic Plus 在值准确率、记录完整性和源追溯性三项指标上均排名第一，且成本远低于编码代理。

Conclusion: ExtractBench 为架构引导提取提供了首个综合性评估框架，有效揭示了当前主流方法的优势与局限，同时展示了 LlamaExtract Agentic Plus 在性能与成本间的优越平衡，推动该技术向实用化迈进。

Abstract: Enterprise workflows increasingly rely on agents for \emph{schema-guided extraction}: given a document and a user-defined schema, the agent faithfully follows the schema to produce the correct output with source evidence as grounding metadata. We present ExtractBench, a benchmark for schema-guided extraction and, to our knowledge, the first to score value accuracy, record completeness at scale, grounding, and measured cost together. The evaluation system contains 4,869 pages across 370 enterprise documents, 8 business domains, and 67 document types, with clear tags differentiating their challenge scenarios. The scalable schema and ground-truth curation pipeline combines independent-system agreement for real documents, known values for synthetic lists, and human verification for forms. We report order-insensitive value F1 for value accuracy, plus two grounding metrics for source traceability: word- and page-level F1. Commercial VLMs perform well on short documents but often truncate record lists on long ones, while coding agents retain higher accuracy at much higher cost. LlamaExtract Agentic Plus ranks first on all three metrics, with accuracy comparable to coding agents at a fraction of the cost. Dataset and evaluation code are available on \href{https://huggingface.co/datasets/llamaindex/ExtractBench}{HuggingFace} and \href{https://github.com/run-llama/ExtractBench}{GitHub}.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [170] [Topology-Aware Data Movement for Disaggregated GPU Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28633)
*Sanjeev Rao Ganjihal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对分布式大模型推理中的KV缓存传输问题，提出一种拓扑感知的传输调度器，通过分层流水传输、NVLink域感知放置和CXL 3.0内存扩展器，显著降低跨GPU池间的传输延迟，在多种架构下相比统一RDMA提升3至18倍性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统在分布式大模型推理中无法正确处理预填充与解码阶段间KV缓存的跨GPU池传输，尤其在70B模型下，单请求传输量达2.6GB，总带宽需求超100GB/s，而现有方案忽略不同物理连接间带宽差异（从900GB/s NVLink到12.5GB/s TCP），导致性能瓶颈。

Method: 设计拓扑感知传输调度器，利用启动时发现的互联层次结构，为每次传输选择最优传输路径；采用分层流水传输以隐藏60%-85%的传输延迟；结合Mixture-of-Experts模型特性，实现专家调度与KV缓存局部性联合优化；引入CXL 3.0内存扩展器作为共享溢出层，提供6倍容量且延迟仅为NVMe的1/86。

Result: 通过分析建模、组件实现与跨三类架构的预测评估，证明该方案可实现3至18倍的传输延迟降低，显著优于传统均匀RDMA策略。

Conclusion: 本文提出的拓扑感知传输调度机制有效解决了大规模分布式推理中因异构网络带来的传输瓶颈，为未来高性能、高扩展性的大模型推理系统提供了关键基础设施支持。

Abstract: Disaggregated LLM inference creates a datacenter networking problem that no existing system solves correctly. When prefill and decode run on separate GPU pools, the KV cache must be transferred between them. For a 70B model this is 2.6 GB per request, exceeding 100 GB/s aggregate at production scale. Yet DistServe, Splitwise, and Mooncake all use uniform RDMA, ignoring that bandwidth between two GPUs varies by 72x depending on their physical relationship: 900 GB/s via NVLink within a domain, 50 GB/s via InfiniBand across nodes, 12.5 GB/s via TCP across data centers. We design a topology-aware transfer orchestrator that discovers interconnect hierarchy at startup and selects optimal transport per transfer. Three mechanisms work together: (1) pipelined layer-by-layer transfer that overlaps transmission with ongoing prefill, hiding 60 to 85 percent of latency behind computation; (2) NVLink domain-aware placement for Mixture-of-Experts models that co-optimizes expert dispatch with KV cache locality; and (3) CXL 3.0 memory expanders as a shared overflow tier providing 6x capacity at 86x lower latency than NVMe. Full evaluation requires multi-node clusters with heterogeneous interconnects and CXL 3.0 hardware that is beyond academic resources and not yet available in GPU clouds. We present analytical bandwidth models, component implementations, and projected analysis across three architectures showing 3 to 18x transfer latency reduction over uniform RDMA.

</details>


### [171] [Sensitivity Analysis of GRU, LSTM and Transformer Encoder in Classification of Automated Driving Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28665)
*Bidhya Shrestha,Christos Papadopoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于车辆遥测数据识别高级驾驶辅助系统（如Comma Openpilot、Tesla Autopilot、Cadillac Super Cruise）的三种序列分类模型（GRU、LSTM、Transformer）的性能与鲁棒性。在干净数据下，三者宏F1得分均超过0.90；在对抗性训练下仍保持0.90以上。作者提出一个模块化鲁棒性评估框架，模拟五类五级遥测数据退化，包括连续信号噪声和事件信号异常。结果表明：事件级扰动影响较小（L5时仍>0.87），但时间抖动导致模型性能急剧下降至0.44-0.50，暴露显著脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为保障自动驾驶系统的安全监控、合规性验证、保险评估与异常检测，需独立监测车辆中实际激活的自动化驾驶系统。现有方法依赖于车载系统自身报告，易受篡改或故障影响。因此，亟需一种不依赖原生系统的独立识别机制，利用车辆遥测数据实现对多种主流自动驾驶系统（包括开源与商业系统）的准确识别与鲁棒性评估。

Method: 采用三种序列建模模型（GRU、LSTM、Transformer encoder）处理车辆遥测数据，进行多类别分类任务。通过真实数据集训练并测试其在正常与受扰状态下的表现。引入模块化鲁棒性评估框架，模拟五种数据退化类型（加性高斯白噪声、累积漂移、跨通道相关噪声、时间抖动；事件信号的突发丢失、延迟跳变、虚假翻转、特征不一致），共五个严重等级（L1-L5）。使用宏F1分数评估各类别平衡下的分类性能。

Result: 在干净数据下，三种模型的宏F1得分分别为：GRU 0.92，LSTM 0.90，Transformer 0.93。对抗训练后，性能略有下降，但仍在0.904–0.916之间。鲁棒性测试显示，事件级扰动对性能影响有限（最低仍高于0.87），而时间抖动导致所有模型性能骤降至0.44–0.50，表现出严重脆弱性。

Conclusion: 基于遥测数据的自动驾驶系统识别在理想条件下具有较高准确性，但对时间抖动等动态扰动极为敏感。未来系统设计需强化对时间同步与信号稳定性要求，并建立更完善的抗干扰机制以保障独立监控的有效性。

Abstract: Automated driving systems (ADSs) are becoming ubiquitous. Future Software Defined Vehicles (SDVs) may be able to run multiple ADSs, both native and aftermarket such as Comma.ai's Openpilot. Monitoring systems to independently verify which automated driving system is active are important for safety monitoring, regulatory compliance, insurance assessment, and anomaly detection. In this paper, we first evaluate the effectiveness of three sequence-based classification models: Gated Recurrent Units (GRU), Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks, and a Transformer encoder model for identifying Level 2 automated driving systems using vehicle telematics data alone: Comma Openpilot, Tesla Autopilot, and Cadillac Super Cruise, along with manual driving. All three models achieve strong clean-data performance with macro F1-scores of 0.92 (GRU), 0.90 (LSTM), and 0.93 (Transformer encoder model) when trained on clean data; threat-matched training yields 0.904-0.916 macro F1 with only a modest clean-data penalty. Second, we introduce a modular robustness evaluation framework that simulates realistic telematics degradation through five corruption families at five severity levels (L1-L5). Continuous channels are perturbed using additive white Gaussian noise with cumulative drift, correlated cross-channel noise, and temporal jitter. Binary event signals are subjected to burst loss, delayed transitions, spurious toggles and cross-feature inconsistencies inspired by communication errors. Robustness is measured using macro-F1, which gives equal weight to each class and is suitable for imbalanced multiclass evaluation. Our evaluation reveals a sharp failure-mode split: event-level corruptions reduce macro-F1 only slightly (greater than equal to 0.87 at L5), while temporal jitter collapses macro-F1 to 0.44-0.50 across GRU, LSTM, and Transformer encoder model.

</details>


### [172] [Guarantees on Dynamical System Distinguishability for LLM Token Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28667)
*Mohamed Akrout,Dan Wilson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究将大语言模型响应分类形式化为两个随机线性动力系统之间的二元假设检验，揭示了即使动态差异显著，其稳态分布的总变差距离也可能极小，从而确立了忽略动态信息的分类器的性能下限。研究进一步证明，基于动力系统的分类错误率随序列长度 $L$ 指数衰减，衰减速率由反映两系统谱距离的动力可区分性量 $δ^2$ 决定。同时，通过引入嵌入模型间的近似交织条件，建立了跨嵌入模型泛化的下界，其依赖于交织映射的最小奇异值。这些理论结果解释了现有方法的实证表现，并推动了利用动力系统理论分析人工智能系统的范式转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于动力系统的方法在分类大语言模型响应方面表现出色，但缺乏理论解释，包括其为何有效、随序列长度如何扩展以及跨不同嵌入模型的迁移能力。

Method: 将分类任务建模为两个随机线性动力系统之间的二元假设检验，利用总变差距离分析稳态分布差异，推导分类错误率随序列长度的指数衰减规律，并引入近似交织条件以分析跨嵌入模型的泛化能力。

Result: 证明了即使动力系统差异显著，其稳态分布的总变差距离仍可任意小，表明忽略动态信息的分类器存在根本性能限制；发现基于动力系统的分类错误率随序列长度指数下降，衰减速率由动力可区分性量 $δ^2$ 控制；建立了跨嵌入模型迁移的下界，与交织映射的最小奇异值相关。

Conclusion: 该研究为基于动力系统的大语言模型分类方法提供了坚实的理论基础，揭示了其性能极限与可扩展性机制，并推动了从‘用AI建模动力系统’向‘用动力系统理论分析AI’的范式转变。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that classifying large language models (LLMs)' responses can be distinguished by modeling token embeddings as trajectories of a black-box dynamical system (DS) and comparing prediction residuals of two DSs. Despite the empirical success of this dynamical approach, a theoretical understanding of why it works, how well it scales as a function of the token sequence, and when it transfers across embedding models remains lacking. We address these questions by formalizing the classification task as a binary hypothesis test between two stochastic linear DSs. We show that the total variation distance between the stationary marginal distributions of the two DSs can be arbitrarily small even when the dynamics differ substantially, which provides a fundamental accuracy floor for any classifier that ignores token dynamics. We then show that the misclassification probability of DS-based classification decays exponentially in the sequence length $L$, with the decay governed by a dynamical discriminability quantity $δ^2$ that captures the spectral distance between the two DSs. We also characterize cross-embedding generalization by introducing an approximate intertwining condition between embedding models and establishing a lower bound on the transferable discriminability in terms of the intertwining map's smallest singular value. Together, these results explain the empirical performance of DS-based classification and motivate further investigation into using DS theory to analyze AI systems, in contrast to the more common approach of using AI to model dynamical systems.

</details>


### [173] [LARA: Lightweight Adapters in the Residual Stream for Composable Adaptation and Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28669)
*Pascal Ekin,Hyosun Choi,Wei Jie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LARA是一种轻量级残差适配方法，通过在冻结模型的残差流中添加低秩修正来实现高效适应，无需修改原始权重。相比LoRA，LARA在代码微调和偏好优化任务中表现相当，且支持平滑的推理时行为插值（γ参数），允许同时驻留多个行为并按令牌自动路由。仅需约33MB额外开销即可在1.5B参数模型上部署7个独立行为，显著优于为每个行为保存完整模型的方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数高效微调方法如LoRA通过修改权重矩阵进行适配，但难以灵活控制行为变化程度，且多行为部署时需要加载多个完整模型，资源开销大。本文旨在提出一种无需修改基础模型权重、支持多行为共存与动态路由、具备平滑行为调节能力的高效适配方法。

Method: LARA在冻结模型的特定层隐藏状态上读取输入，通过低秩矩阵生成残差修正，并将其加回残差流，从而实现对输出的调整；所有基础权重保持不变，新增行为以独立的低秩模块形式存在，可通过一个可学习的缩放因子γ在推理时动态控制行为强度，支持多行为共享同一基础模型并按令牌选择使用。

Result: 在代码微调和偏好优化（DPO）任务中，LARA性能与等参数量的LoRA相当；可在单个1.5B模型上部署7个不同行为，总额外开销仅约33MB，远低于为每个行为保存完整模型的方案；支持平滑的行为插值与按令牌自动路由，适合多行为共存与动态部署场景。

Conclusion: LARA提供了一种高效、灵活、可扩展的模型适配范式，其基于残差流的修改方式避免了权重更新，支持多行为共存、动态路由与连续行为调控，在资源受限设备上具有显著优势。

Abstract: We present LARA (Lightweight Additive Residual Adaptation), a method for efficient adaptation that operates in the residual stream of a frozen model rather than in its weights. Where LoRA adds an update of low rank to weight matrices, LARA reads the hidden state at a small set of layers and adds a correction of low rank back to the residual stream, leaving all base weights untouched. On a code fine-tuning task and on preference optimization (DPO), LARA matches LoRA at equal parameter counts. Because adaptation is a frozen base plus a residual, LARA exposes a scale γ, applied at inference, that interpolates smoothly between base and adapted behavior, a form of graded control that adaptation in weight space does not offer. Finally, because each behavior is a small residual module over a shared frozen base, many behaviors can be held resident at once and routed automatically per token. We place seven behaviors, six fine-tuned and one optimized for preference, on one frozen 1.5B model for roughly 33 MB of overhead, against one full model for each behavior. Because the base is untouched, behaviors are trained separately and selected per token rather than loaded on demand, which suits hosting many behaviors, and adding new ones, on a single model on a device.

</details>


### [174] [Hierarchical Copula-Gumbel-Top-\texorpdfstring{$K$}{K} Routing: Two-Sided Dependence Control for Frozen Mixture-of-Experts at Fixed Per-Token Routing Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28670)
*Richard Yi Da Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种名为Hierarchical Copula-Gumbel-Top-K（CGA）的双面构造方法，用于在保持每个令牌路由分布不变的前提下，控制不同令牌间路由选择的依赖关系。通过组内正相关（高斯耦合）增强专家集的一致性，跨组负相关（反向构造）降低负载方差。该方法在不改变基础模型的情况下，利用可训练控制器通过得分函数估计器实现动态调节，验证了机制可行性，但尚未证明任务层面的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE模型中独立路由导致专家负载波动大、组内一致性不足；需要在保持单个令牌路由分布不变的前提下，引入可控的依赖结构以提升专家使用效率与负载均衡性。

Method: 提出Hierarchical Copula-Gumbel-Top-K（CGA）框架：组内使用交换性高斯耦合来正相关各令牌的Gumbel扰动，增强组内专家选择一致性；跨组采用可调反向构造引入负相关，降低整体负载方差。所有操作均在预路由logits条件下保持原始路由分布不变，且仅通过控制器进行可微调参数更新。

Result: 实验表明，组内正相关可提升专家集一致性，但会增加实际负载方差；跨组负相关可降低负载方差，且在相同组内强度下优于平滑耦合。两种依赖机制共同构成在不变约束下的可调控平衡系统，实现了对一致性与负载分散性的双重控制。

Conclusion: CGA框架在不修改基础模型的前提下，通过可训练控制器实现对路由依赖结构的精细调控，有效平衡了专家选择的一致性与负载分布的稳定性，为MoE系统的优化提供了新工具。

Abstract: A stochastic Gumbel-Top-$K$ router defines, for every token of a mixture-of-experts (MoE) model, a \emph{routing law}: a distribution over ordered expert lists and mixture weights. We ask which \emph{joint} distributions over the routing choices of different tokens are reachable while every individual token's complete routing law is held exactly fixed. We give a two-sided construction, \emph{Hierarchical Copula-Gumbel-Top-$K$} (\CGA{}). Within a group of related tokens, an exchangeable Gaussian copula positively correlates the Gumbel perturbations at each expert coordinate, which can increase within-group expert-set coherence. Across disjoint pairs of groups, a tunable antithetic construction introduces a selectable amount of negative dependence. We prove that both operations leave each token's ordered Top-$K$ sample, mixture weights, and inclusion probabilities identical in distribution to independent routing \emph{at a routing layer conditioned on its pre-routing logits}; conditional expected expert traffic is preserved as a consequence. We characterize the resulting trade-off: positive within-group coupling can only inflate the variance of realized expert loads relative to independent routing, while nonnegative cross-group opposition can only reduce it relative to flat coupling at the same within-group strength. Coherence and load dispersion are thus controlled by two complementary dependence dials on the invariance constraint surface. Because the base model is untouched, the dials can be driven by a small controller over frozen features, trainable with a score-function estimator: the frozen network is evaluated only in the forward direction, and gradients are confined to the controller. An initial small-scale pilot validates the mechanism and the training route, but does not establish task-level fine-tuning gains.

</details>


### [175] [LAWFUL: Law-Aligned Witness for Faithful Use of Latents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28672)
*Kevin Chen,Kenneth W. Parker,Anish Arora*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出LAWFUL框架，旨在解决神经网络在连续变量上学习物理定律时的四个可解释性缺口，包括因果一致性度量、有效域测试、守恒律与禁止行为验证以及物理量流动量化。通过在Mocap2Radar变换器上的应用，验证了模型是否从运动捕捉和雷达数据中学习并内部使用多普勒频率定律 $f(t) = \frac{2 v(t)}{\lambda}$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前神经网络在预测物理系统时，难以确认其是否真正学习并内化了物理定律作为结构化知识，尤其在连续变量上缺乏对因果一致性、有效域、守恒律及物理量流动的可解释性分析。

Method: 提出LAWFUL框架，引入覆盖感知的因果一致性度量与有效域测试机制，结合反事实分析与电路追踪技术，评估神经网络内部是否遵循物理定律。

Result: 在Mocap2Radar变换器上成功验证了模型在未显式提供频率与速度信息的情况下，学习并内用了多普勒频率定律 $f(t) = \frac{2 v(t)}{\lambda}$，且其内部计算符合该定律的结构与行为约束。

Conclusion: LAWFUL框架为理解神经网络如何学习和使用物理定律提供了可解释性的基础工具，推动了深度学习在科学建模中的可信应用。

Abstract: When a neural network predicts a physical system accurately, has it learned the governing law as formal, structured knowledge, and if so, does the network's internal computation actually use that representation throughout the law's domain of validity? We identify four interpretability gaps that limit answering these questions for {\em physics laws over continuous variables}: the absence of a coverage-aware causal-consistency measure over continuous counterfactuals; of a domain-of-validity test for the identified circuit; of a verification of the law's invariants and forbidden behaviors; and of a quantification of how a derived physical quantity flows through the circuit. We develop a foundational framework, LAWFUL, that closes the first two and lays groundwork for the remaining two, and illustrate it on the Mocap2Radar transformer, validating whether it learns and internally uses the Doppler frequency law $f(t) = \frac{2 v(t)}λ$ from motion-capture and radar data in which neither $f(t)$ nor $v(t)$ appears.

</details>


### [176] [MPP-GNN: Subject-Adaptive Community Detection for fMRI-Based Alzheimer's Disease Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28681)
*Yang Zhang,Xiao Zhou,Jonathan Warrell,Avram Holmes,Xuan Zhang,Mark Gerstein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MPP-GNN模型，通过耦合双层优化实现自适应图分割，发现个体特异性功能模块，并利用这些模块作为先验指导边精炼和表征学习，提升阿尔茨海默病分类性能，且与Yeo脑图谱功能网络组织高度一致，揭示AD的网络去分化特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN方法在分析脑功能连接时假设所有受试者具有预设的功能模块数量，忽略了个体间差异；同时发现的模块未能直接指导连接模式的学习。

Method: 提出元概率池化图神经网络（MPP-GNN），采用耦合双层优化框架，分层进行自适应图划分以发现个体特异性模块，并将模块作为显式先验用于引导边的精炼和表示学习。

Result: 在两个公开数据集上进行阿尔茨海默病分类，MPP-GNN的表现优于现有基线模型，获得最高AUC值；且其结果与Yeo脑图谱的典型功能网络组织高度一致，揭示了阿尔茨海默病的网络层面去分化模式。

Conclusion: MPP-GNN有效解决了个体间功能模块差异问题，通过模块引导提升了模型性能与可解释性，为脑疾病分类提供了更精准、更具生物学意义的分析框架。

Abstract: Functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI) is a widely used technique for studying the brain. Recent methods that utilize graph neural networks (GNNs) for analysis of brain functional connectivity have shown great potential for the classification of brain disorders, such as Alzheimer's disease (AD). However, these methods often assume a preset number of functional modules across all subjects, which overlooks inter-subject variability. In addition, the discovered modules are rarely used to directly guide the learned connectivity patterns. Here, to address these issues, we propose a Meta Probabilistic Pooling GNN (MPP-GNN). We frame the model's task as a coupled, bilevel optimization that performs adaptive graph partitioning hierarchically to discover subject-specific modules and then uses the discovered brain modules as an explicit prior to guide edge refinement and representation learning. We validate MPP-GNN on two public datasets for AD classification, achieving the highest AUC in comparison to established baselines for both datasets. Furthermore, our analysis demonstrates that MPP-GNN shows significant alignment with the canonical functional-network organization defined by the Yeo brain atlas and reveals a network-level dedifferentiation pattern for AD.

</details>


### [177] [Technological Advances in Detecting and Managing Cognitive Impairment in Older Adults: Trends, Challenges, and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28687)
*Mohammad Asif,Azizuddin Khan,Mohd Azam,Anurag Rajkumar Bombarde*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了用于检测和管理老年人认知障碍的最新技术进展，涵盖脑电图（EEG）、神经影像学（MRI、淀粉样蛋白/tau PET）、血液生物标志物及数字标记，并通过人工智能（AI）、机器学习（ML）和深度学习（DL）实现整合。提出跨学科分类体系、方法学严谨性标准、早期检测框架及多类比较表格。尽管EEG标志物与深度模型表现优异，但多数研究基于小样本、单中心数据，缺乏外部验证；而血浆p-tau217已具备临床应用价值，2025年首个血液检测获批辅助阿尔茨海默病诊断；抗淀粉样蛋白药物（lecanemab、donanemab）虽疗效有限且有争议，但已获批；多领域生活方式干预趋于成熟。可穿戴设备、远程监测、语音与虚拟现实技术推动持续生态化评估，多模态融合提升诊断准确性。主要挑战包括标准化、可解释性、数据隐私及公平可推广部署。未来关键在于建立可信、多模态、纵向验证的系统，实现早期发现与个性化干预的衔接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着人口老龄化加剧，从轻度认知障碍（MCI）进展为痴呆已成为未来数十年的重大健康挑战，但常规评估常错过早期迹象。亟需发展更敏感、可靠、可推广的早期检测与干预技术体系，以应对这一全球性公共卫生问题。

Method: 采用批判性综合方法，整合神经生理信号（如EEG）、结构与分子神经影像（MRI、PET）、血液生物标志物及数字标记，结合人工智能、机器学习与深度学习技术进行分析；构建跨学科分类体系，提出方法学严谨性标准，设计多层级筛查-干预框架，并制作对比表格以系统梳理各类检测方法、干预措施及风险/保护因素。

Result: EEG标志物（如α/θ波变化、P300潜伏期）与深度学习模型（如CNN、LSTM/BiLSTM、Transformer、自监督EEG基础模型）显示出高准确率；血浆p-tau217已进入临床实用阶段，2025年首款相关血液检测获批；抗淀粉样蛋白疗法获得批准，但疗效存在争议；多领域生活方式干预已趋成熟；可穿戴设备与数字工具支持连续、生态有效监测；多模态融合显著提升诊断敏感性和特异性。然而，仍面临数据标准化、模型可解释性、隐私保护及公平部署等挑战。

Conclusion: 认知障碍的早期检测与管理正迈向可信、多模态、纵向验证的智能系统时代。未来核心在于构建能够将早期发现与个性化、可行动的临床干预无缝衔接的整合平台，推动精准预防与个体化医疗的发展。

Abstract: As populations age, cognitive decline from mild cognitive impairment (MCI) to dementia is a defining health challenge of the coming decades, yet routine assessment often misses its earliest signs. This article critically synthesizes recent technological advances for detecting and managing cognitive impairment in older adults, spanning neurophysiological signals (chiefly electroencephalography, EEG), structural and molecular neuroimaging (MRI and amyloid/tau PET), blood-based biomarkers, and digital markers, integrated through artificial intelligence (AI), machine learning (ML), and deep learning (DL). Beyond summarizing, it contributes a cross-disciplinary taxonomy, a methodological-rigor lens foregrounding subject- and site-independent validation, an integrative early-detection framework linking tiered screening to intervention, and comparison tables of detection methods, interventions, and risk and protective factors. EEG markers (alpha/theta changes, P300 latency) and deep models (CNNs, LSTM/BiLSTM, transformers, self-supervised EEG foundation models) report strong accuracy, yet many rest on small, single-site datasets unlikely to survive rigorous external validation. Elsewhere, gains are tangible: plasma p-tau217 has reached clinical utility, with the first blood test cleared to aid Alzheimer's diagnosis in 2025; anti-amyloid therapies (lecanemab, donanemab) are approved despite modest, contested benefits; and multidomain lifestyle prevention has matured. Wearable, remote, speech, and virtual-reality tools enable continuous, ecologically valid monitoring, and multimodal fusion improves sensitivity and specificity. Barriers remain: standardization, explainability, data privacy, and equitable, externally validated deployment. The field's near-term promise lies in trustworthy, multimodal, longitudinally validated systems linking early detection to actionable, personalized care.

</details>


### [178] [SEDR-Seq2P: A Lightweight Dilated Residual Sequence-to-Point Network for Multi-Task Industrial NILM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28693)
*Hatem Haddad,Feres Jerbi,Issam Smaali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对工业级非侵入式负载监测（NILM）中因测量噪声和多设备并发运行导致模型泛化能力下降的问题，提出一种一对多、多任务分解设置，通过单一网络估计多个工业设备的负荷。在IMDELD数据集上，对比了Seq2Seq、Seq2SubSeq、Seq2Point、GRU和WaveNet等模型，发现Seq2Point在准确率与延迟之间平衡更优，但GRU和WaveNet虽精度更高，计算开销显著增加。为此，提出轻量级改进模型SEDR-Seq2P，引入扩张残差块与挤压-激励注意力机制，在保持低延迟的同时，将MAE降低约7%，提升决定系数约1%，匹配率提高约0.8%。相比WaveNet，推理延迟降低约58%，展现出更优的精度-延迟权衡，适合大规模工业部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业级非侵入式负载监测面临测量噪声和多设备同时运行带来的挑战，现有基于住宅数据训练的模型在工业场景下泛化能力差；同时，高精度模型如WaveNet存在计算开销大、延迟高的问题，难以满足工业场景对实时性与可扩展性的要求。因此需要设计一种兼具高精度与低延迟的轻量级模型以支持工业级部署。

Method: 采用一对多、多任务学习框架，使用单一神经网络从总功率信号中同时估计多个工业设备的负载。对比多种序列建模结构（如Seq2Seq、Seq2Point、GRU、WaveNet），并在此基础上提出SEDR-Seq2P模型，结合扩张残差块增强特征提取能力，引入挤压-激励注意力机制提升关键特征权重，从而在不增加过多计算负担的前提下提升性能。

Result: SEDR-Seq2P相比基准的Seq2Point，MAE降低约7%，决定系数提升约1%，匹配率提高约0.8%；相较于WaveNet，推理延迟降低约58%，实现了更高的精度与更低的延迟，具备良好的实用性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: SEDR-Seq2P是一种高效、轻量且适用于工业环境的非侵入式负载监测模型，其在保证高精度的同时显著降低推理延迟，为工业级大规模部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Industrial NILM remains challenging because measurement noise and widespread concurrent machine operation reduce the generalization of models tuned on residential data. This work adopts a one-to-many, multi-task disaggregation setting, in which a single network estimates multiple industrial machine loads from aggregate power. Under a unified evaluation protocol on IMDELD, we benchmark Seq2Seq, Seq2SubSeq, Seq2Point, GRU, and WaveNet using energy-estimation metrics and the accuracy-delay criterion. While Seq2Point offers a stronger accuracy-delay balance than Seq2Seq/Seq2SubSeq, GRU and WaveNet achieve higher accuracy at markedly higher computational cost. To close this gap, we propose SEDR-Seq2P, a lightweight Seq2Point extension with dilated residual blocks and squeeze-and-excitation attention. Relative to the Seq2Point baseline, SEDR-Seq2P reduces MAE by approximately 7%, improves the coefficient of determination by approximately 1%, and increases the match rate by approximately 0.8%. In addition, compared to WaveNet, SEDR-Seq2P reduces inference latency by approximately 58%, yielding a favorable accuracy-delay trade-off for scalable industrial deployment.

</details>


### [179] [Predicting Steel Fatigue Life from Micrographs Using Physics-Informed Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28695)
*Aryuemaan Kumar Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CV框架，通过光学显微图像无损预测轻质合金钢的疲劳寿命（log N_f），结合OpenCV预处理、28维物理信息特征提取和CNN回归模型，使用GNLL损失联合预测寿命与不确定性。在合成数据上，ResNet-50表现优异（R²=0.93，RMSE=0.18 log-cycles，宏F1=0.91），且校准误差降低76%；推理速度<65ms/图，代码与数据集开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统疲劳寿命测试耗时长（数十至数百小时），不适用于快速质量控制；需发展无需物理测试即可从显微图像预测疲劳寿命的新方法。

Method: 采用七阶段OpenCV预处理去除伪影，设计28维物理信息特征提取器（涵盖裂纹形貌、晶粒结构、孔隙率与纹理），结合基于GNLL损失的CNN回归模型，实现对log N_f及样本特异性不确定性的联合预测。

Result: 在合成微图基准上，ResNet-50达到R²=0.93，RMSE=0.18 log-cycles，宏F1=0.91；GNLL相比MSE使预期校准误差下降76%（ECE: 0.089→0.021）；Grad-CAM显示网络关注冶金学上有意义的微结构特征。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了快速、高精度的疲劳寿命预测，具备良好的可解释性与计算效率，适用于工业快速质检；未来需开展真实现场样本的域迁移验证。

Abstract: Here is the plain text version optimized for arXiv's submission form. Custom macros (like \CV and \SI) have been converted to standard text/math so they render correctly on the webpage: Evaluating the fatigue life of structural steels conventionally requires mechanical testing lasting tens to hundreds of hours, making it impractical for rapid quality control. We present CV, a computer vision framework that estimates the fatigue life ($\log N_f$) of lightweight alloy steels directly from optical micrographs without physical testing.The pipeline features a seven-stage OpenCV preprocessing routine to remove artifacts, a 28-dimensional physics-informed feature extractor (quantifying crack morphology, grain structure, porosity, and texture), and a CNN regression model trained with a Gaussian negative log-likelihood (GNLL) loss to jointly predict $\log N_f$ and sample-specific uncertainty $\hatσ$.Evaluating three architectures (SE-CNN, ResNet-50, VGG-16) on a synthetic micrograph benchmark, ResNet-50 achieves $R^2 = 0.93$, RMSE = 0.18 log-cycles, and macro-F1 = 0.91. The GNLL objective reduces Expected Calibration Error by 76% compared to a mean-squared-error baseline (ECE: $0.089 \rightarrow 0.021$). Grad-CAM maps confirm the network attends to metallurgically meaningful microstructural features.Running in under 65 ms per image, the pipeline and synthetic dataset generator are open-sourced. Because validation relies entirely on synthetic micrographs, these results demonstrate methodological soundness under simulated conditions; a domain-transfer study on real field samples is the immediate next step.

</details>


### [180] [Mitigating Class-Tail Undercoverage in Medical Vision-Language Models under Clinical Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28696)
*Mushir Akhtar,M. Tanveer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CALCoDe is a post-hoc reliability layer for frozen medical vision-language models (VLMs) that ensures both marginal and worst-class coverage under clinical shifts. It combines localized conformal thresholds with class-tail adaptive thresholds via a one-sided maximum, while using cross-fitted validation to identify high-risk classes and an independent support audit for deferral. CALCoDe achieves 0.95+ coverage across all eight evaluated settings, outperforming existing methods.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing conformal methods fail to address class-wise undercoverage in medical VLMs under clinical shifts, especially when source prevalence is unreliable due to varying acquisition protocols and model backbones. There's a need for a method that simultaneously handles localized uncertainty and tail-class risks.

Method: CALCoDe uses cross-fitted validation to detect classes at risk of undercoverage, estimates class-conditional tail thresholds on a disjoint calibration split, and combines these with localized conformal thresholds using a one-sided maximum. An independently calibrated support audit defers cases with insufficient inlier support, ensuring finite-sample coverage under exchangeability.

Result: In all eight evaluated settings (two dermatology shifts across four VLM backbones), CALCoDe achieves both marginal and worst-class accepted coverage of at least 0.95. On HAM10000 to ISIC 2019, it attains 0.970 average worst-class coverage—surpassing sTACP (0.926) and LCP-VLM (0.864).

Conclusion: CALCoDe effectively mitigates class-wise undercoverage in medical VLMs under distributional shifts by adaptively protecting tail classes while preserving localization benefits, making it the first method to consistently achieve high marginal and worst-class coverage across diverse clinical settings.

Abstract: Medical vision-language models (VLMs) can retain high observed marginal coverage after clinical shift while substantially under-covering an individual disease class. The affected class varies with acquisition protocol and backbone geometry, so source prevalence does not reliably reveal the failure. Existing localized and tail-aware conformal methods respectively adapt to test neighborhoods and source-frequency tails, leaving held-out class-wise coverage failure unmodeled. We introduce Class-Tail Adaptive Localized Conformal Deferral (CALCoDe), a post-hoc reliability layer for frozen medical VLMs. Cross-fitted validation predictions identify classes at risk of undercoverage, and a disjoint calibration split estimates their class-conditional tail thresholds. CALCoDe combines each protected threshold with a localized conformal threshold using a one-sided maximum. The resulting set contains every label admitted by the localized rule, with additional protection confined to validation-identified classes. An independently calibrated support audit defers cases with insufficient inlier support. Under exchangeability among accepted examples within each protected class, CALCoDe provides finite-sample coverage at the prespecified guard level and contains the corresponding localized conformal sets; coverage on shifted external cohorts is evaluated empirically. Among standard conformal baselines and recent VLM-specific conformal methods evaluated across two dermatology shifts (HAM10000 to ISIC 2019 and HAM10000 to PAD-UFES-20) and four frozen VLM backbones (BiomedCLIP, OpenAI CLIP ViT-B/32, PubMedCLIP ViT-B/32, and MedSigLIP-448), CALCoDe is the only approach whose observed marginal and worst-class accepted coverage both reach 0.95 in all eight settings. On HAM10000 to ISIC 2019, its average worst-class accepted coverage is 0.970, compared with 0.926 for sTACP and 0.864 for LCP-VLM.

</details>


### [181] [Flow Matching with Missing Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28698)
*Fairoz Nower Khan,Nabuat Zaman Nahim,Peizhong Ju*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出缺失数据流匹配方法，将缺失坐标视为潜在变量，通过对可能取值平均流匹配损失。理论上证明该修正为精确而非近似，在随机缺失且真实补全条件下，不完整数据目标函数等于完整数据目标函数，学习内容不受缺失影响，难题转移到补全模型。有限样本分析揭示设计问题的非直观答案：缺失仅转移估计方差而非增加，每样本一个补全即可达到完整数据方差，固定评估预算下单个补全最优；学习的补全模型引入单一不可约偏差，其上界为条件Wasserstein距离。实验验证理论预测，表明确定性而非冻结插补导致生成分布坍塌，并在真实表格数据上与经典及深度插补基线表现相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界应用中训练数据常存在缺失，而传统流匹配假设数据完全可观测，因此需要一种能处理缺失数据的流匹配方法，以提升实际应用能力。

Method: 将缺失坐标视为潜在变量，对可能取值平均流匹配损失，通过理论推导证明该方法在特定条件下可保持学习目标不变，并结合有限样本分析指导算法设计。

Result: 理论和实验均验证了方法的有效性：缺失仅转移方差、单次补全即达最优、学习补全模型引入可控偏差；实验显示确定性插补会破坏生成分布，且本方法性能优于或媲美现有主流插补方法。

Conclusion: 缺失数据流匹配方法在理论上精确且有效，能正确处理缺失数据，其性能关键在于补全模型的设计，且在真实数据上表现优异，为高维缺失数据建模提供了新思路。

Abstract: Flow matching assumes fully observed training data, which many real-world applications rarely provide. We propose Missing-Data Flow Matching, which treats the missing coordinates of training samples as latent variables and averages the flow matching loss over the values they could take. We first prove the correction is exact rather than approximate. Under missing completely at random with true completions, the incomplete-data objective equals the complete-data objective, so missingness changes nothing about what flow matching learns and the entire difficulty relocates to the completion model. Our finite-sample analysis then answers design questions that the algorithm leaves open, and the answers are not the ones intuition suggests. Missingness transfers estimator variance rather than adding it, one completion per example already matches complete-data variance exactly, and under a fixed evaluation budget one completion is optimal. A learned completion model contributes a single irreducible bias, which we bound by its expected conditional Wasserstein distance to the true completion law. Experiments numerically validate the theoretical predictions, show that deterministic rather than frozen imputation is what collapses the generated distribution, and place our method alongside strong classical and deep imputation baselines on real tabular data.

</details>


### [182] [MMFGU: Multimodal Federated Graph Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28708)
*Haodong Lu,Zekai Chen,Weiwei Ji,Shihao Li,Xunkai Li,Xun Wu,Yinlin Zhu,Rong-Hua Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MMFGU框架，解决多模态联邦图学习中的细粒度删除请求问题，通过目标特定表示解耦实现高效、安全的去学习，显著提升效率并保持图模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦图去学习方法难以应对多模态场景下的细粒度删除需求，如删除特定图像或文本但保留实体，或撤销模态间关联，需解决精准删除、防止信息恢复和避免残留传播等问题。

Method: MMFGU框架通过将异构删除请求统一为目标载体，利用目标特定表示解耦分离待删与保留语义，结合轻量探针暴露并修复传播残余，通过紧凑原型与响应信号选择性清除受影响客户端，实现高效去学习。

Result: 实验表明，MMFGU能有效移除请求内容，保持图模型可用性，相比全量重训练提速41.5倍。

Conclusion: MMFGU为多模态联邦图学习提供了高效、安全的去学习解决方案，支持细粒度、多样化的数据删除请求，兼顾隐私保护与模型性能。

Abstract: Multimodal federated graph learning enables clients to collaboratively train graph models over structural, textual, and visual signals without sharing private local data. However, the presence of heterogeneous multimodal content also makes unlearning requests more frequent and fine-grained: users may delete accounts or interactions, remove a particular image or text while retaining the associated entity, or revoke the learned correspondence between retained modalities or graph attributes. Existing federated graph unlearning mainly handles entity/relation or client removal and cannot directly satisfy these multimodal requests. They introduce three challenges: removing only the requested information without damaging retained content, preventing the target from being recovered through remaining modalities or graph neighborhoods, and stopping related traces on other clients from re-entering the global model after aggregation. To address them, we propose \textsc{\textbf{MMFGU}}, a multimodal federated graph unlearning framework built around target-specific representation decoupling. \textsc{MMFGU} maps heterogeneous requests into unified target carriers, decouples requested representations while anchoring retained semantics, exposes and repairs propagated residuals with lightweight probes, and selectively purges affected clients through compact prototype and response signals. Experiments show that \textsc{MMFGU} effectively removes requested information, preserves retained graph utility, and achieves a $\boldsymbol{41.5\times}$ speedup over full retraining.

</details>


### [183] [Mirror Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28737)
*Yunpeng Liu,Matthew Niedoba,Oluwanifemi A. Adekanye,Jason Yoo,Yingchen He,Berend Zwartsenberg,Frank Wood*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为镜像学习（mirror learning）的框架，通过第三人称观察来实现模仿学习。该方法利用微调的视频扩散模型进行视角转换，将学习者置于示范者的视角，并结合逆动力学模型推断学习者控制空间中的动作轨迹，从而合成伪第一人称专家数据（镜像数据）。实验表明，仅使用镜像数据即可训练出有效策略，且在第一人称行为克隆基础上加入镜像数据可进一步提升策略性能。结果表明，现代生成式世界模型隐含足够结构，可提供一种可扩展且安全的替代方案，减少对遥操作数据采集的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统行为克隆（BC）在密集且对齐的第一人称数据上表现良好，但无法有效利用人类和动物日常使用的第三人称示范中丰富的观察信号。为解决这一问题，本文旨在探索如何从被动观察中学习可执行策略，突破对人工标注或遥操作数据的依赖。

Method: 提出镜像学习框架，包含两个核心组件：(i) 通过微调视频扩散模型实现视角转换，使学习者获得示范者的视角；(ii) 使用逆动力学模型推断学习者自身控制空间中的动作轨迹，从而合成镜像数据（即伪第一人称专家数据）。

Result: 实验验证了镜像数据单独即可训练出有效策略；在第一人称行为克隆的基础上引入镜像数据，显著提升了下游策略性能。表明生成式世界模型具备足够的结构信息，支持高效、安全的数据生成方式，可降低对昂贵遥操作数据的依赖。

Conclusion: 镜像学习提供了一种利用第三人称观察进行模仿学习的新范式，借助生成式模型合成高质量训练数据，为减少人工数据标注和遥操作依赖提供了可行路径，具有良好的可扩展性和安全性。

Abstract: We investigate imitation learning through the lens of third-person observation and propose a framework for mirror learning: acquiring actionable policies from passive observation. While behavior cloning (BC) excels under dense, well-aligned first-person data, it fundamentally fails to leverage the rich observational signals arising from third-person demonstrations that humans and animals routinely exploit. We introduce a method that composes (i) a learned perspective transformation that places learners in demonstrators' shoes using a fine-tuned video diffusion model and (ii) an inverse dynamics model that infers action trajectories in the learners' control space. This enables the synthesis of mirror data, pseudo first-person expert data generated from third-person observations of demonstrator behavior. Empirically, we show that mirror data alone can train effective policies, and that augmenting first-person BC training with mirror data further improves downstream policy performance. Our results suggest that modern generative world models implicitly encode sufficient structure to enable a scalable and safe alternative to teleoperation-heavy data collection.

</details>


### [184] [TAGTorch: A PyTorch Library for Geometry, Topology, and Symmetry-Aware Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28755)
*Brendan Kennedy,Tegan Emerson,Gregory Roek,Emilie Purvine,Henry Kvinge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了TAGTorch，一个基于PyTorch的开源库，旨在统一受拓扑、代数和几何启发的工具，涵盖数据预处理、模型架构、训练技术和模型分析等，以解决现有软件生态碎片化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前神经网络在具有丰富几何、拓扑或对称结构的数据应用中日益广泛，但相关算法的软件支持仍分散且缺乏维护，许多方法仅以研究原型形式存在，难以推广使用。

Method: 提出并实现TAGTorch，一个整合拓扑、代数与几何相关工具的PyTorch库，涵盖数据预处理、模型设计、训练方法和模型分析等功能模块，并强调其设计哲学与可扩展性。

Result: TAGTorch提供了一个统一、可维护、易扩展的软件平台，填补了现有工具链中的空白，有助于推动拓扑与几何驱动的神经网络方法的广泛应用与持续发展。

Conclusion: TAGTorch为拓扑、代数与几何相关的深度学习研究提供了坚实而统一的软件基础，未来将持续拓展功能并增强社区支持。

Abstract: Over the last decade, neural networks have been applied to an increasingly diverse range of applications, including data with rich geometric, topological, or symmetry-related structure. As a result, researchers have increasingly drawn inspiration from topology, algebra, and geometry. Despite this rich algorithmic development, the supporting software ecosystem remains fragmented. Many important methods exist only as research prototypes in unmaintained repositories. We address this by introducing Topology, Algebra, and Geometry Torch (TAGTorch), an open-source, PyTorch-based library that unifies tools inspired by topology, algebra, and geometry, including data-preprocessing methods, architectures, training techniques, and model analysis tools. We describe the design philosophy of TAGTorch and then discuss its current architecture and capabilities, highlighting areas where it can fill gaps in the current software ecosystem. We conclude with a discussion of our future development priorities for the library.

</details>


### [185] [Feature Interaction Modeling for Physics-Informed Neural Networks and Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28762)
*Quan Gu,Hongxia Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究将因子分解机（FM）中的特征交互模块嵌入物理信息神经网络（PINNs）和神经算子学习中，以增强对参数化偏微分方程（PDEs）解流形的模型表达能力。通过利用多元函数的二阶泰勒展开来刻画变量耦合，提出了FM-PINN，显式捕捉时空变量间的交互，提升了光滑高阶PDE的逼近精度。进一步地，将空间坐标、时间、物理参数及初边值条件划分为独立特征组，并建模其跨组交互，由此发展出FM-Operator和FM-DeepONet，对非线性守恒律及具有陡峭梯度或间断的问题尤为有效，但在平滑算子学习基准上无一致优势。数值实验表明，该机制在冲击主导的复杂方程上显著提升精度，为强跨场依赖的参数化PDE的物理一致性建模提供了有前景的方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提升对参数化偏微分方程（PDEs）解流形的建模能力，特别是处理具有强变量耦合、非线性、尖锐梯度或间断性的复杂问题，需增强模型的表达能力。现有方法在捕捉多变量交互方面存在局限，因此引入因子分解机（FM）中的特征交互机制，以更有效地建模跨空间、时间、参数及边界条件之间的复杂依赖关系。

Method: 提出FM-PINN，将因子分解机的二阶特征交互结构嵌入物理信息神经网络；进一步构建FM-Operator与FM-DeepONet，通过将空间坐标、时间、物理参数、初始与边界条件划分为独立特征组，并显式建模其跨组交互，从而实现对复杂非线性系统中多场耦合关系的精确刻画。

Result: 在冲击主导的非线性守恒律等挑战性问题上，所提方法显著提升了预测精度；在光滑的参数化PDE问题上表现稳定但无明显优势；整体验证了特征交互机制在强跨场依赖场景下的有效性与潜力。

Conclusion: 通过引入因子分解机的特征交互机制，显著增强了物理信息神经网络与神经算子模型对复杂参数化偏微分方程的建模能力，尤其适用于具有强烈变量耦合、间断或剧烈变化的系统，为未来物理一致建模提供了一条高效且可扩展的新路径。

Abstract: This work embeds feature interaction modules derived from factorization machines (FMs) into physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) and neural operator learning, to enhance model expressiveness for solution manifolds of parameterized partial differential equations (PDEs). Motivated by the second-order Taylor expansion of multivariate functions to characterize variable couplings, we first propose FM-PINN. It explicitly captures spatio-temporal variable interactions and improves the approximation accuracy for smooth high-order PDEs. We further group spatial coordinates, time, physical parameters, and initial and boundary conditions into independent feature sets and model their cross-group interactions. Based on this strategy, we develop FM-Operator and FM-DeepONet, which are particularly effective for nonlinear conservation laws and problems with sharp gradients or discontinuities, while offering no consistent advantage on smooth operator learning benchmarks. Numerical tests demonstrate that the proposed mechanism delivers substantial accuracy gains on challenging shock-dominated equations, indicating a promising direction for physics-consistent modeling of parameterized PDEs with strong cross-field dependencies.

</details>


### [186] [Representations from Pretrained Machine-Learning Interatomic Potentials as Coarse Coordinates for Material Generation and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28776)
*Paul Hagemann,Katharina Ueltzen,Simon Müller,Janine George,Philipp Benner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨了基于预训练机器学习原子间势（MLIPs）如MACE的原子平均特征在无机晶体结构生成中的应用。提出了一种新的评估方法——粗-细运输距离（CFTD），通过结合两种不同特征提取器，能够统一评估生成结构的质量与新颖性。该方法在检测模型记忆现象方面表现优异，并优于近期提出的连续SUN指标。此外，研究还展示了粗粒度MACE特征可作为生成模型的指导信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生成式机器学习在无机晶体结构生成中广泛应用，但多数模型依赖于简单的晶体结构表示方式，导致评估和生成质量受限。为提升评估精度与生成性能，需要更先进的特征表示和评价框架。

Method: 提出并使用基于MACE的原子平均特征作为核心特征表示；引入粗-细运输距离（CFTD）作为分布基础的评估框架，结合粗粒度特征衡量质量，利用细粒度特征捕捉新颖性；同时将粗粒度特征用于引导生成模型。

Result: CFTD能有效评估晶体结构的质量与新颖性，成功识别出模型的记忆化问题，且性能优于连续SUN指标；使用粗粒度MACE特征作为引导可显著提升生成结构的质量与多样性。

Conclusion: 基于预训练MLIPs的原子平均特征在晶体结构生成与评估中具有强大潜力，所提出的CFTD框架为生成模型提供了一个高效、全面的评估工具，同时可作为生成过程的有效引导信号。

Abstract: Generative machine learning is increasingly used for inorganic crystal structure generation. Most models and the corresponding evaluation approaches rely on simple forms of crystal structure representation. In this paper, we showcase the power of atom-averaged features from pretrained Machine-Learning Interatomic Potentials (MLIPs), such as MACE, for such tasks. We first introduce a distance measure that assesses the output of material generative models by capturing both quality and novelty in a single distribution-based evaluation framework. In particular, we introduce the Coarse-Fine Transport Distance (CFTD) using two different featurizers, where the quality component is based on coarse MACE features. We showcase CFTD's versatility in capturing crystal-structure quality while also detecting memorization, and compare it with the recently introduced continuous SUN metrics. We further show that coarse MACE features can be used as guidance for a material generative model.

</details>


### [187] [Distilling Knowledge from Large Language Models into Lightweight Reinforcement Learning Agents for Autonomous Cyber Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28826)
*Konur Tholl,François Rivest,Mariam El Mezouar,Adrian Taylor,Ranwa Al Mallah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究如何利用大语言模型（LLM）提升自主网络防御决策能力，通过提示工程使一个80亿参数的预训练网络安全LLM在修改版CybORG CAGE挑战环境中表现优于基线强化学习（RL）代理。进一步提出一种在线策略蒸馏框架，将LLM的防御策略迁移到仅含64,910参数的轻量级RL代理中，显著降低模型规模但保持有效防御能力。实验在4至12台主机的多种网络配置下验证了方法的可迁移性，并发现奖励驱动的RL优化与教师指导策略之间存在对齐局限，无法持续超越优化后的教师策略。结果表明，面向网络安全的LLM可作为自主防御的专家知识来源，而策略蒸馏为将前沿模型部署于高效、可扩展的实用化代理提供了可行路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自主网络防御系统依赖强化学习（RL）代理进行决策，但其训练过程需大量探索，导致初期行为不稳定且决策能力差。为提升初始防御性能并实现高效部署，亟需引入更高效的专家知识源和轻量化模型架构。

Method: 采用提示工程而非微调的方式激活预训练的80亿参数网络安全领域大语言模型（LLM），将其作为防御策略的“教师”；设计在线策略蒸馏框架，将LLM的策略迁移到小型轻量级强化学习代理中；通过构建不同规模的CybORG网络场景评估策略的可迁移性与稳定性。

Result: LLM在未微调情况下即表现出优于基线RL代理的防御性能；所提出的策略蒸馏框架成功将模型参数减少数个数量级，同时保持有效防御能力；在多种网络配置下验证了方法的鲁棒性和可迁移性；但基于奖励驱动的RL优化未能一致超越教师策略，揭示了策略对齐的挑战。

Conclusion: 网络安全领域的大型语言模型可作为自主防御系统的有效专家知识来源，结合策略蒸馏技术，能够实现从高复杂度模型到轻量级、可部署代理的高效转化，为实际应用提供可行路径。

Abstract: Autonomous Cyber Operations (ACO) are increasingly important for defending enterprise networks as cyber threats continue to evolve in sophistication. ACO applications commonly employ Reinforcement Learning (RL) agents to learn defensive behaviors through interaction with environments. However, RL agents typically require extensive exploration during training, often resulting in unstable behavior and poor initial decision-making before converging toward effective defense strategies.
  In this work, we investigate the use of a Large Language Model (LLM) to improve autonomous defensive decision-making within an ACO environment. Through prompt engineering rather than fine-tuning, we demonstrate that an 8-billion parameter LLM pretrained on cybersecurity data can outperform a baseline RL agent in a modified CybORG CAGE Challenge 2 environment. We then propose an online policy distillation framework that transfers the LLM's defensive policy into a lightweight RL agent containing only 64,910 parameters, reducing model size by several orders of magnitude while maintaining effective defensive capabilities. This provides a pathway toward operationalizing frontier cybersecurity models within lightweight, deployable agents.
  To evaluate transferability, we construct CybORG scenarios ranging from 4 to 12 hosts and assess the approach across varying network configurations. We also evaluate teacher-guided RL stabilization strategies and observe that none consistently surpass the optimized teacher policy, suggesting policy-alignment limitations between reward-driven RL optimization and teacher-guided defense strategies.
  Our results demonstrate the potential of cybersecurity-focused LLMs as sources of expertise for autonomous cyber defense, while policy distillation provides a practical path toward operationalizing frontier cybersecurity models within efficient, scalable agents.

</details>


### [188] [Hypergradient-based Bilevel Reinforcement Learning with Improved Sample Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28849)
*Naman Saxena,Mudit Gaur,Vaneet Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于超梯度的无海森矩阵（Hessian-free）双层强化学习算法，利用玻尔兹曼策略在熵正则化折扣强化学习目标函数下的最优性。该算法具有O(ε⁻¹)的迭代复杂度和最先进的$	ilde{O}(ε^{-2})$样本复杂度，并在收敛性分析中去除了先前研究中对外层目标函数的Polyak-Lojasiewicz (PL)条件假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双层强化学习算法或因使用包含海森矩阵的超梯度而不可扩展，或因采用基于惩罚的近似方法而导致高样本复杂度。本文旨在解决这些问题，设计一种高效且可扩展的双层强化学习算法。

Method: 基于熵正则化的折扣强化学习目标函数，利用玻尔兹曼策略的最优性，提出一种无需海森矩阵的超梯度方法，实现高效的双层优化。

Result: 算法达到O(ε⁻¹)的迭代复杂度和$	ilde{O}(ε^{-2})$的样本复杂度，在温和正则性条件下表现优异；同时在收敛性分析中不再依赖PL条件，提升了理论适用范围。

Conclusion: 所提算法在效率、可扩展性和理论完备性方面均优于现有方法，为双层强化学习提供了新的有效解决方案。

Abstract: Bilevel reinforcement learning (RL) is an important framework within the literature of RL that can be used to formalize various categories of problems, such as meta-learning, hierarchical task decomposition, and reinforcement learning from human feedback (RL-HF). Most of the bilevel RL algorithms are either not scalable because of using hypergradient with Hessian, or they suffer from high sample complexity because of using penalty-based approximation methods. In this work, we propose a hypergradient-based bilevel RL algorithm using the optimality of the Boltzmann policy for the entropy regularized discounted RL objective function. Our proposed algorithm is Hessian-free and obtains an iteration complexity of $O(ε^{-1})$ and state-of-the-art sample complexity of $\tilde{O}(ε^{-2})$ under mild regularity conditions. Further, in our convergence analysis, we are able to remove the assumption of the Polyak-Lojasiewicz (PL) condition on the outer-level objective function present in the prior state-of-the-art sample complexity work.

</details>


### [189] [An analysis of machine learning approaches for enhancing decision-making in complex discrete choice tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28854)
*Sheng Lun Christine Cao,Destenie Nock,Alex Davis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究评估了四种机器学习模型（多项式逻辑回归、广义加性模型、孪生神经网络和高斯过程）在学习和预测五种重要决策规则（线性强效用、单调强效用、理想点、字典序半序和多属性线性球形累加器）方面的表现。通过蒙特卡洛实验，考察了属性数量、训练选择集数量和决策规则确定性对模型性能的影响。结果显示，半参数和非参数模型在所有决策规则和实验情境中普遍优于参数模型；随着训练选择集和决策规则确定性的增加，模型性能分别提升6%至96%和0%至55%。真实能源政策偏好数据的案例研究显示，孪生神经网络（TNN）表现最佳（BIC=13.351）。研究证实了半参数与非参数模型在政策导向离散选择建模中的可行性与局限性，并强调应根据任务情境选择合适模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统离散选择建模依赖于参数模型，但其在个体异质性背景下的表现受限。机器学习可提供数据驱动方法以更好地捕捉个体偏好，然而其在复杂偏好结构下的表现尚不明确，尤其在实际偏好收集中面临的挑战下。因此，亟需评估机器学习模型在不同条件下的建模能力，以指导政策制定中的偏好推断。

Method: 采用蒙特卡洛模拟实验，系统评估四种机器学习模型在不同设置下的表现：包括改变选择选项的属性数量、训练选择集的数量以及决策规则的确定性水平。同时，基于真实能源政策偏好数据进行案例研究，比较各模型的拟合优度（以BIC值衡量），验证模型在真实场景中的适用性。

Result: 半参数和非参数模型整体表现优于参数模型，且模型性能随训练样本量和规则确定性的增加而显著提升。孪生神经网络（TNN）在真实数据案例中表现最佳，具有最低的BIC值（13.351），表明其在复杂偏好结构中具备较强的建模能力。

Conclusion: 半参数与非参数机器学习模型在政策导向的离散选择建模中具有较高可行性和优势，尤其适用于存在个体异质性和复杂决策规则的情境。模型选择应依据具体任务特征，如数据规模、规则复杂度和确定性水平，以实现最优预测性能。

Abstract: Discrete choice modeling is a common tool used for preference elicitation during policy-making, but this is typically done through parametric models. Machine learning can push the boundaries of discrete choice modeling for policy-based preference elicitation by adopting a data-driven approach or learning individual preferences. However, there is limited knowledge of how well machine learning methods can estimate individual discrete choice rules under individual heterogeneity, especially in the context of challenges often experienced during preference elicitation. This study evaluates four machine learning models (multinomial logistic regression, generalized additive model, twinned neural network, and Gaussian process) with respect to their capacity to learn and predict five choice rules that are important in the behavioral and social sciences (linear strong utility, monotonic strong utility, ideal point, lexicographic semiorder, and multiattribute linear ballistic accumulator). Monte Carlo experiments were performed to assess model performance when increasing a) the number of attributes in the choice alternatives, b) the number of training choice sets, and c) the choice rule's determinism. The simulation results demonstrated that semi-parametric and non-parametric models generally outperform parametric models across all choice rules and experimental contexts. Model performance also generally improves by 6% to 96% and 0% to 55%, respectively, with an increase in training choice sets and choice rule determinism. A case study using real energy policy preference data was also conducted, where TNN performed best with a BIC of 13.351. This work demonstrated the viability and limitations of semi-parametric and non-parametric models in the context of policy-centric discrete choice modeling and showed how the choice task context should drive model selection.

</details>


### [190] [Fast Rates for Swap-Agnostic Learning of Proper Losses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28856)
*Princewill Okoroafor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了交换无关学习（swap-agnostic learning），提出了一种针对固定适当损失的离线学习算法，实现了在有限假设类 $H$ 和光滑适当损失下的 $\widetilde{O}((\log |H|/m)^{2/3})$ 超额风险，并在在线场景下获得 $\widetilde{O}(T^{1/3}(\log |H|)^{2/3})$ 的交换后悔界。对于整个损失族，还给出了统一的算法，其在线和离线误差率分别为 $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{T\log |H|})$ 与 $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{\log |H|/m})$，在凸、1-利普希茨损失下进一步提升至 $\widetilde{O}(T^{1/3}(\log |H|)^{2/3})$ 和 $\widetilde{O}((\log |H|/m)^{2/3})$，均达到最优对数因子意义下的紧致性。核心技术是通过伯恩斯坦风格的方差修正将交换无关学习归约为二阶多校准问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统异构学习要求在每个预测值上独立解决一个学习问题，计算开销大；本文旨在设计更高效的方法，使预测依赖的后处理可联合控制，从而实现更优的泛化性能与计算效率。

Method: 通过黑尔方法（Blackwell approachability）结合伯恩斯坦风格的方差修正，将交换无关学习问题转化为一种二阶形式的多校准问题，进而设计出高效的离线与在线学习算法。

Result: 提出了新的交换无关学习算法，显著优于已有结果，实现了紧致的在线与离线误差率，且适用于多种损失函数族。

Conclusion: 本文证明了交换无关学习可以通过联合控制实现，避免逐点求解，其提出的算法在理论性能上达到最优对数因子意义下的紧致性，为预测依赖后处理提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Swap-agnostic learning strengthens classical agnostic learning by allowing the comparator to select a different hypothesis on each level set of the learner's predictions. This benchmark captures prediction-dependent postprocessing, but appears to require solving a separate agnostic-learning problem for every possible prediction value. We show that, for proper losses, these prediction-level comparisons can instead be controlled jointly. Our main result is an offline swap-agnostic learner for any fixed proper loss. For a finite hypothesis class $H$ and any fixed smooth proper loss, the excess risk from $m$ i.i.d. samples is $\widetilde{O}((\log |H|/m)^{2/3})$, with a corresponding online swap-regret bound of $\widetilde{O}(T^{1/3}(\log |H|)^{2/3})$. We also give algorithms whose predictions are simultaneously swap-agnostic for entire families of losses. For all proper losses bounded in $[-1,1]$, we obtain online and offline rates of $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{T\log |H|})$ and $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{\log |H|/m})$, respectively. For convex, $1$-Lipschitz proper losses, these rates improve to $\widetilde{O}(T^{1/3}(\log |H|)^{2/3})$ online and $\widetilde{O}((\log |H|/m)^{2/3})$ offline. These bounds are tight up to logarithmic factors and improve upon the $\widetilde{O}(T^{2/3}(\log |H|)^{1/3})$ rate implied by the swap-omniprediction guarantee of Luo et al. (2025). Our main technical contribution is a reduction from swap-agnostic learning to a second-order form of multicalibration, obtained via Blackwell approachability with a Bernstein-style variance correction.

</details>


### [191] [Adaptivity via a Parallel Architecture for Stochastic Gradient Methods Adaptivity via a Parallel Architecture for Stochastic Gradient Methods Adaptivity via a Parallel Architecture for Stochastic Gradient Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28902)
*Bin Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种并行框架，通过组合静态梯度方法实现更好的自适应性。该框架利用多个处理器并行搜索最优迭代次数 $T$，基于几何序列选择参数，每个处理器在每阶段执行指定迭代次数的梯度下降，以满足收敛条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统静态梯度方法缺乏自适应性，难以根据问题特性动态调整步长和迭代次数；为提升优化效率与收敛性能，需设计一种能够自动适应问题结构的并行优化框架。

Method: 采用 $p$ 个处理器并行运行多个静态梯度方法 $\mathrm{GD}(x_0, T_{j,i})$，其中 $T_{j,i}$ 由预定义函数 $h(j,i)$ 决定，各处理器在不同阶段执行不同迭代次数的梯度下降，通过几何序列搜索最优 $T$ 以满足收敛条件。

Result: 该框架在理论上保证了更好的收敛性与自适应能力，且在并行环境下显著提升了优化效率，尤其适用于大规模机器学习问题。

Conclusion: 所提出的并行框架有效结合了静态梯度方法的稳定性与并行计算的优势，实现了对优化过程的更好自适应，具备良好的理论基础与实际应用潜力。

Abstract: We develop a parallel framework that assembles static gradient methods to achieve better adaptivity. A static gradient method, denoted by $\mathrm{GD}(x_0,T)$, takes as input an initial point $x_0\in\mathbb{R}^n$ and $T\in \mathbb{R}^+$ specifying the number $\floor{T}$ of iterations. The step size is chosen as $s=S(T)$, where $S(\cdot)$ is a predetermined function of $T$. The method then performs the iterations $ x_{i+1}=x_i-\fracη{s}\cdot g_i,$ where $g_i$ is a stochastic gradient evaluated at $x_i$, and $η$ is a scaling factor. For an integer $p\ge1$, the $p$ processors in the proposed parallel framework search for an appropriate value of $T$ according to a geometric sequence so that the resulting gradient descent satisfies the desired convergence conditions. Each processor executes an infinite sequence of stages indexed by $i=1,2,\ldots$. At stage $i$, processor $j$ is assigned $ T_{j,i}=h(j,i),$ where $h:\mathbb{N}\times\mathbb{N} \rightarrow\mathbb{R}^{+}$ is a prescribed function. Processor $j$ $(j=0,1,\ldots,p-1)$ executes $\mathrm{GD}(x_0, T_{j,i})$ at stage $i$.

</details>


### [192] [Reflection or Re-Generation? Why LLM Revision Fails Where Human Revision Succeeds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28908)
*Yefan Tao,Gerald Friedland,Madhusudhanan Chandrasekaran,Luyang Kong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出人类-大语言模型反思框架（HRF），通过双阶段协议在自反思、同伴和跨代理设置下对比人类与LLM的修订行为。基于信息论分析发现，LLM反思存在两种失效模式：在客观任务中信息增益近乎为零，表现为无差别的重生成；在主观任务中信息增益为负，使预测偏离目标。而人类修订在两类任务中均产生正向增益。跨代理实验表明失败源于修订步骤本身，而非输入质量。诊断分析显示不同任务和模型中主导机制各异，无统一错误检测机制。核心结论是：缺乏外部信息时，自条件修订无法降低对目标的不确定性，因此LLM反思更应被理解为条件重生成，而非真正的基于错误驱动的修订。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大语言模型（LLM）的‘反思’是否真正模拟人类的修正过程，揭示其在实际应用中的局限性与机制缺陷。

Method: 构建人类-大语言模型反思框架（HRF），采用两阶段控制实验，在自反思、同伴和跨代理场景下比较人类与LLM的修订行为；使用信息论方法（每轮交叉熵减少量）量化信息增益，并通过诊断分析（基于首阶段正确性的修订、基于预言信号的修订等）识别关键子步骤。

Result: LLM在客观任务中几乎无信息增益（ΔI≈0），在主观任务中出现负增益（ΔI<0），而人类始终有正增益；即使面对高质量人类回答，LLM也会在修订中退化；不同任务和模型中错误检测与修复机制表现不一，无统一规律；根本原因在于缺乏外部信息，导致自条件修订无法有效降低不确定性。

Conclusion: LLM的反思本质上是条件重生成，而非真正的错误驱动修订。其效果受限于结构设计，无法通过内部自洽实现有效修正，需引入外部信息或监督机制以提升修订能力。

Abstract: Reflection, the ability to revisit and revise prior reasoning, is central to how humans improve their answers. Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly prompted to "reflect," yet whether this resembles human revision remains unclear. We introduce the Human-LLM Reflection Framework (HRF), a controlled two-pass protocol comparing human and LLM revision under identical conditions across self-, peer-, and cross-agent settings. Using an information-theoretic analysis based on per-iteration cross-entropy reduction, we find two failure modes of LLM reflection. On objective tasks with finite answer spaces, reflection yields near-zero information gain (Delta I approx 0), behaving as neutral re-generation indistinguishable from re-sampling. On subjective tasks, it yields significant negative gain (Delta I < 0), moving predictions away from the target. Human revision, by contrast, yields positive gain in both settings. Cross-agent experiments localize the failure to the revision step, not input quality: LLMs degrade even high-quality human responses. Diagnostic analyses (revision conditioned on first-pass correctness, and oracle-guided revision against a random-reshuffle baseline) show that which sub-step dominates varies by task and by model rather than reducing to a single mechanism: self-error detection is present on objective multiple-choice tasks but weak on subjective ones, and recovery under an oracle error signal exceeds the baseline for some models and falls below it for others. The unifying account is structural: without external information, self-conditioned revision cannot reduce uncertainty about the target, so LLM reflection is better understood as conditioned re-generation than as genuine error-driven revision.

</details>


### [193] [Gated Q-learning: Add Off-Policy Bias to Taste](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28916)
*Brett Daley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Gated Q-learning introduces a novel framework that resolves the long-standing trade-off between off-policy bias and credit assignment in Q-learning by using a state-action-dependent gating mechanism, enabling longer eligibility traces without importance sampling. It provides theoretical guarantees and empirical results showing faster learning with reduced bias.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The paper addresses the fundamental challenge in reinforcement learning of balancing off-policy bias and effective credit assignment in Q-learning, which has historically required choosing between severely truncated traces (Watkins' Q(λ)) or introducing detrimental errors (Peng's Q(λ)). Modern methods fail due to collapsing importance-sampling ratios under greedy policies.

Method: Gated Q-learning employs a continuous, exploration-aware gating mechanism to selectively attenuate eligibility traces, avoiding reliance on importance sampling. The approach is theoretically grounded, proving contraction mapping properties and deriving exact fixed points.

Result: Empirical evaluations show that intermediate gating enables longer credit-assignment horizons, leading to faster initial learning compared to both traditional extremes, while maintaining stability and reducing off-policy bias.

Conclusion: Gated Q-learning offers a simple, effective alternative to importance sampling, allowing customizable multistep horizons and controlled off-policy bias, thus resolving a decades-old dilemma in Q-learning.

Abstract: Multistep credit assignment is critical for sample-efficient reinforcement learning, yet managing off-policy bias in Q-learning remains a fundamental challenge. For 30 years, practitioners have been limited to a binary choice: eliminate the bias at the cost of severely truncated eligibility traces (Watkins' Q($λ$)), or ignore the bias to learn faster while injecting detrimental errors into the value estimates (Peng's Q($λ$)). Modern off-policy estimators fail to resolve this tension, as importance-sampling ratios collapse under Q-learning's greedy target policy. We introduce Gated Q-learning, a novel algorithmic framework that ends this dilemma by smoothly interpolating between the two historical extremes. Rather than relying on importance sampling, our approach employs a continuous, state-action-dependent gating mechanism to selectively attenuate eligibility traces in an exploration-aware manner. We provide a rigorous theoretical foundation for this mechanism, proving that the expected operator remains a contraction mapping and deriving its exact fixed point. Empirical evaluations verify that intermediate gating safely enables longer credit-assignment horizons, yielding faster initial learning than either extreme. Gated Q-learning offers a simple alternative to importance sampling while enabling customization of the effective multistep horizon and the amount of off-policy bias in Q-learning agents.

</details>


### [194] [Learning Optimal Dynamic Matching via Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28925)
*Genta Okada,Shunya Noda,Junpei Komiyama,Akira Matsushita*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于价值的强化学习框架，用于处理动态匹配市场中的匹配时机与对象选择问题。通过事件时间约简，证明规划者可在每次外生事件后立即决策并等待下一次事件，从而简化策略设计。最优边级Q函数可由后决策残差图上的单一延续值函数刻画，将学习目标从状态-动作值简化为图值。采用图神经网络近似该值函数，并通过时序差分学习训练，结合前向贪心匹配启发式进行决策。在二元类型基准测试中，所学策略显著优于即时和阈值贪心规则，能有效保留常见节点以应对稀有高价值匹配机会；在肾脏配对捐赠案例中，当退出不可预测时表现接近即时贪心，在预警可靠时恢复患者匹配逻辑，并在中间预警概率下超越最优的即时贪心与患者贪心策略。结果表明，残差图值学习可生成依赖状态的动态匹配策略，适应实际连通性和退出信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态匹配市场面临核心权衡：立即匹配可获得当前价值，但会移除未来可能产生更高价值的参与者。现有方法难以有效平衡短期收益与长期机会，尤其在参与者随时间演化、连接关系随机且存在外生退出的复杂场景中。因此亟需一种能够自适应实时信息变化、兼顾当前与未来收益的智能匹配策略。

Method: 构建无限期连续时间模型，涵盖随机到达、节点类型转移、边生成及外生退出。提出事件时间约简定理，证明最优策略只需在每个外生事件后立即行动。进一步揭示最优边级Q函数可由后决策残差图上的单一延续值函数表征，将学习目标从高维状态-动作空间降维至图值。采用图神经网络近似该值函数，利用时序差分学习进行训练，并结合前向贪心匹配启发式实现近似最优动作选择。

Result: 在二元类型基准中，所提策略显著优于即时匹配和阈值贪心策略，能有效保留常见节点以捕捉稀有高价值匹配机会，仅在池子较厚时才形成低价值匹配。在肾脏配对捐赠案例中，当退出不可预测时性能接近即时贪心；当预警可靠时，能准确反映患者匹配逻辑；在中间预警概率下，超越即时贪心与患者贪心中的更优者。整体表明该方法能根据实际连通性与退出信息自适应调整匹配行为。

Conclusion: 通过残差图值学习，本研究成功实现了状态依赖的动态匹配策略，能够有效融合实时网络结构与退出信息，提升匹配效率与长期价值，为复杂动态匹配市场提供了一种高效、可扩展的智能决策框架。

Abstract: Dynamic matching markets require decisions about whom to match and when: matching now yields value but removes participants who may create better future opportunities. We develop a value-based reinforcement-learning framework for this problem on finite, evolving weighted graphs. We study an infinite-horizon continuous-time model with stochastic arrivals, node-type transitions, edge realizations, and exogenous exits. We prove an event-time reduction: without loss of optimality, the planner acts immediately after each exogenous event and then waits for the next one. We further show that the optimal edge-wise $Q$-function is characterized by a single continuation-value function on post-decision residual graphs, reducing the learned object from state-action values to graph values. Exact action selection still requires combinatorial matching optimization; we approximate the value with a graph neural network, train it by temporal-difference learning, and use it in a forward-greedy matching heuristic. In a binary-type benchmark, the learned policy substantially outperforms immediate and threshold-greedy rules by preserving common nodes for rare arrivals of valuable matches while forming lower-value matches only in thick pools. In a kidney paired donation benchmark, it performs similarly to immediate greedy when exits are unpredictable, recovers the logic of patient matching when warnings are reliable, and outperforms the better of Immediate Greedy and Patient Greedy across intermediate warning probabilities. These results show that residual-graph value learning yields state-dependent dynamic matching policies that adapt to realized connectivity and exit information.

</details>


### [195] [Latent Lie-Poisson Neural Networks (LLPNNs): Discovering the motion of Lie-Poisson systems through observable data and latent dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28939)
*Vakhtang Putkaradze*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为LLPNN的结构保持框架，用于从可观测数据中学习李-泊松动力学，适用于正则和退化的哈密顿系统。该方法利用哈密顿解码器或伪拉格朗日编码器、由李-泊松对称性降低产生的普遍诺特定律以及基于Magnus的李群更新来重建可观测与隐变量动态，实现了长期预测的高精度和强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多重要的力学和控制中的哈密顿系统可通过李-泊松系统进行对称性降低，但其动力学在通常不可观测的动量变量上演化，而实际数据仅包含配置和速度等可观测量；此外，在最优控制问题中，隐变量包含不可观测的共态，且哈密顿量可能退化，导致无法构造对应拉格朗日量，使传统编码器-解码器方法失效。因此需要一种能直接从可观测数据学习李-泊松动力学的结构保持方法。

Method: 引入了隐式李-泊松神经网络（LLPNN），结合三种几何要素：(i) 在主动变量上学习哈密顿解码器或伪拉格朗日编码器；(ii) 通过李-泊松对称性降低产生的普遍诺特定律构建隐变量轨迹；(iii) 利用李-泊松流与基于Magnus的李群更新重建可观测和隐变量动力学。

Result: 在广义刚体在SO(3)上的运动、基尔霍夫水下车辆在SE(3)上的运动以及多个交互车辆在SE(2)^N上的最优控制问题三个示例中，数值实验表明该方法具有出色的长期预测精度、对噪声的强鲁棒性，并且仅需少量数据和轻量级神经网络架构即可达到竞争性性能。

Conclusion: LLPNN是一种有效的结构保持方法，能够直接从可观测数据中学习李-泊松系统的动态，适用于广泛存在的正则与退化哈密顿系统，为复杂物理系统的数据驱动建模提供了新范式。

Abstract: Structure-preserving neural networks are essential for the long-term prediction of Hamiltonian systems from data. Many important Hamiltonian systems in mechanics and control admit symmetry reduction to Lie--Poisson systems, including rigid bodies, underwater vehicles, fluids, plasmas, and optimal control problems. A fundamental challenge in learning such systems is that their dynamics evolve in momentum variables that are typically unobservable, while available data consist only of observable quantities such as configurations and velocities. In optimal control applications, the situation is further complicated because the latent variables contain unobservable co-states and the Hamiltonian may be degenerate, preventing the existence of a corresponding Lagrangian and rendering the encoder-decoder approaches inapplicable.
  We introduce Latent Lie--Poisson Neural Networks (LLPNNs), a structure-preserving framework for learning Lie--Poisson dynamics directly from observable data. The proposed approach exploits three geometric ingredients: (i) learning either a Hamiltonian decoder or a pseudo-Lagrangian encoder on the active variables, (ii) constructing latent trajectories through a universal Noether invariant arising from Lie--Poisson symmetry reduction, and (iii) reconstructing observable and latent dynamics through Lie--Poisson flows combined with Magnus-based Lie-group updates. The resulting method preserves the geometric structure and is applicable to both regular and degenerate Hamiltonian systems.
  We demonstrate the method on three examples: a generalized rigid body on SO(3), Kirchhoff's underwater vehicle on SE(3), and an optimal-control problem for interacting vehicles on $SE(2)^N$. Numerical experiments show excellent long-term predictive accuracy, strong robustness to noise, and competitive performance using only modest datasets and lightweight neural-network architectures.

</details>


### [196] [FairDiffuseVQVAE: Sampling-Time Fairness in Tabular Diffusion via Conditional Refinement of Vector-Quantized Latents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28945)
*Nitish Nagesh,Mahdi Bagheri,Amir M. Rahmani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FairDiffuseVQVAE 是一种两阶段架构，通过解耦数据保真度与公平性，实现高保真度合成数据的同时显著提升公平性。第一阶段使用无公平性项的向量量化自编码器与行级判别器，第二阶段采用基于分类器自由引导的连续扩散模型，条件依赖于第一阶段重建结果和受保护属性。推理时对受保护属性进行均匀采样，可自然实现人口均等性，无需额外损失项。在 Adult、Bank 和 COMPAS 数据集上，其平均人口均等比率达 0.702（较 FairTabDDPM 提升 47%），平等机会比率达 0.686（提升 100%），且配对相关误差最低（0.034），仅牺牲约 15 AUC 点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格式扩散模型（如 TabDDPM、TabSyn）虽具备优异的分布保真度，但缺乏公平性机制；而公平性感知生成模型（如 DECAF、FairTGAN、FairTabDDPM）在训练中施加显式公平性惩罚，导致样本质量或下游效用显著下降。亟需一种既能保持高质量生成又能有效提升公平性的方法。

Method: 提出两阶段框架 FairDiffuseVQVAE：第一阶段为无公平性项的向量量化自编码器（VQ-VAE）结合行级判别器，用于学习高质量数据表示；第二阶段为类 DiffuseVAE 的连续扩散模型，通过分类器自由引导条件于第一阶段重构结果及受保护属性，实现精细化修正。推理时对受保护属性均匀采样，自动满足人口均等性约束。

Result: 在 Adult、Bank、COMPAS 数据集上，FairDiffuseVQVAE 实现最高平均人口均等比（0.702，+47% 相较 FairTabDDPM）与平等机会比（0.686，+100%）；配对相关误差最低（0.034），优于所有已有方法；仅牺牲约 15 AUC 点以换取公平性提升。

Conclusion: FairDiffuseVQVAE 成功实现保真度与公平性的解耦，通过设计合理的两阶段生成流程与推理策略，在不损害整体数据质量的前提下显著提升公平性表现，为隐私保护数据共享与公平机器学习提供了有力工具。

Abstract: Synthetic tabular data is increasingly used in privacy-preserving data sharing, data augmentation, and to mitigate downstream classifier bias. State-of-the-art tabular diffusion models such as TabDDPM and TabSyn achieve excellent distributional fidelity but offer no mechanism for fairness; conversely, fairness-aware tabular generators (DECAF, FairTGAN, FairTabDDPM) impose explicit fairness penalties at training time, yielding modest fairness gains at substantial cost to either sample quality or downstream utility. We introduce FairDiffuseVQVAE, a two-stage architecture that decouples fidelity from fairness: a vector-quantized autoencoder with a row-level discriminator (Stage~1, no fairness terms) is followed by a DiffuseVAE-style continuous diffusion refiner that conditions on both the Stage-1 reconstruction and the protected attribute via classifier-free guidance (Stage~2). Fairness emerges as a property of the sampling distribution -- uniform sampling of the protected attribute at inference time enforces demographic parity by construction, rather than from competing loss terms. On the Adult, Bank and COMPAS datasets, FairDiffuseVQVAE achieves the highest mean Demographic Parity Ratio ($0.702$, $+47\%$ over FairTabDDPM) and Equalized Odds Ratio ($0.686$, $+100\%$). It also attains the lowest mean pair-wise correlation error ($0.034$) of any published method, while explicitly trading $\sim$$15$ AUC points for these fairness gains.

</details>


### [197] [Shapley-Value-Based Feature Attribution for Data Masking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28946)
*Xinxue,Qu,Francis Bilson Darku,Hong Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种基于Shapley值的特征归因方法，用于解决数据隐私中的披露风险与数据效用之间的权衡问题。与以往在数据集层面考虑该权衡的研究不同，该框架在特征层面进行分析，并且对数据掩码方法、统计或机器学习方法以及评估指标具有无关性。实验结果表明，该方法能够在保持数据效用的同时有效降低披露风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个人数据的广泛获取带来了严重的隐私担忧，现有研究多关注数据集层面的风险-效用权衡，但缺乏在特征层面的公平归因方法，因此需要一种更细致、灵活且通用的框架来解决数据隐私问题。

Method: 采用基于Shapley值的特征归因方法，从披露风险和数据效用两个维度出发，对每个特征进行公平评估，从而实现特征级别的隐私保护优化。该框架不依赖特定的数据掩码技术、建模方法或评估指标。

Result: 实验验证了该框架能有效降低数据披露风险，同时较好地保留数据的实用性，证明其在实际应用中的有效性与普适性。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架为数据隐私保护提供了一个新的、可推广的特征级视角，能够兼顾公平性与实用性，适用于多种数据处理场景。

Abstract: Despite its many benefits, widespread access to individuals' personal data also causes severe privacy concerns for consumers, companies, and policymakers. This study proposes a novel framework that adapts the Shapley-value-based feature attribution approach to the problem domain of data privacy by capturing the two crucial dimensions of data privacy---disclosure risk and data utility. Our proposed framework takes a holistic view of data masking through a fair feature attribution approach based on Shapley values. Different from the existing literature that mostly focuses on the risk-utility tradeoff at the dataset level, the proposed framework addresses the tradeoff at the feature level. Furthermore, the proposed framework is agnostic to data masking methods, statistical and machine learning methods, and data utility and disclosure risk evaluation metrics. Experimental results show that our proposed method can effectively reduce disclosure risk while preserving data utility.

</details>


### [198] [Overcoming the Weakest-Link Effect in LLM-Driven Program Optimization via Heterogeneous Edit Recombination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28947)
*Jingwen Fu,Zhen Liu,Yuhan Liu,He Zhang,Nanning Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HERO is a program optimizer that uses LLMs to generate diverse, non-overlapping atomic edits and selects them based on evaluator scores, avoiding the weakest-link effect in zeroth-order search. It outperforms prior methods in finding high-scoring programs faster and with fewer tokens.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing LLM-based program optimizers rely on textual gradients that become unreliable as program-score mappings grow complex. The paper argues that explicit gradients are not necessary and proposes a zeroth-order optimization strategy using direct atomic edits from LLMs.

Method: HERO prompts an LLM to generate diverse, non-overlapping atomic edits from the current program, then evaluates and composes these edits using evaluator scores to form coherent improvements, mitigating the weakest-link effect.

Result: HERO achieves higher-scoring programs and faster convergence across algorithmic problems, strategy games, agentic system design, and robotic path planning, while using fewer tokens than prior LLM-based optimizers.

Conclusion: Explicit gradients are not essential for effective program optimization. HERO demonstrates that LLM-driven zeroth-order search, when properly structured, can significantly outperform gradient-based approaches in complex program spaces.

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to solve complex problems by searching over program space, offering a general paradigm for scientific problems that can be naturally represented and solved as programs. Despite recent progress, identifying effective optimization directions for a candidate program remains challenging. By analogy with automatic differentiation, existing methods typically guide the search using a textual ``gradient'': a first-order update direction expressed as textual edits. Such gradients are inferred either from previously evaluated programs or from LLM-generated feedback on the implicit program-score mapping. However, these estimates become increasingly unreliable as the program--score mapping grows more complex, limiting their practical utility. We argue that explicit gradients are not essential for effective program optimization. Leveraging their prior knowledge, LLMs can propose plausible atomic edits directly from the current program, thereby enabling a zeroth-order optimization strategy. However, zeroth-order search suffers from a \textit{weakest-link effect}: when a bundle of edits is accepted or rejected as a whole, a single harmful edit can negate the benefits of all remaining edits. To address this issue, we introduce HERO, a program optimizer that prompts an LLM to generate diverse, non-overlapping atomic edits and then systematically selects and composes them into coherent program improvements using evaluator scores. We evaluate HERO across algorithmic problems, strategy games, the design of LLM-based agentic systems, and robotic path planning. Across these domains, HERO consistently discovers higher-scoring programs and converges substantially faster than prior LLM-based optimizers, while consuming fewer tokens.

</details>


### [199] [Mining Verdict Boundaries for Neural Network Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28954)
*Jiawei Ren,Guanqin Zhang,Zhenya Zhang,Yulei Sui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种改进的分支定界（BaB）方法，通过利用路径单调性并同时分割多个激活函数（如ReLU），实现对神经网络验证中判决边界的有效搜索。该方法在每条路径上进行指数级搜索，跳过大量无关子问题，显著提升效率；进一步通过量化信息估计边界位置，优化验证过程。实验表明，该方法在常用基准测试中优于现有BaB方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统BaB方法在深度递增的树路径上逐个求解昂贵的子问题，导致大量计算开销，尤其在每个节点都需要进行代价高昂的边界传播，效率低下。因此亟需更高效的判决边界定位策略。

Method: 提出基于路径单调性的并行多激活函数分割策略，采用指数级搜索跳过无关子问题，并引入量化信息辅助边界位置估计，以提升搜索效率与精度。

Result: 实验结果显示，所提方法在多个标准基准测试中显著优于现有BaB方法，在保证完整验证的前提下大幅减少推理时间与计算资源消耗。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的高效搜索机制有效解决了传统BaB方法中的低效问题，为大规模神经网络的完整验证提供了更实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Branch and Bound (BaB) aims to achieve complete verification of neural networks by adaptively partitioning the problem and applying off-the-shelf verifiers to subproblems. Its problem-splitting history can be represented as a tree, where each subproblem corresponds to a child node. A key problem of BaB lies in searching for the verdict boundaries across all the paths that divide the verified and unverified subproblems. We observe that the existing BaB approach tackles this problem by solving each expensive subproblem sequentially along the tree path as its depth increases, requiring costly bounds propagation at every visited BaB tree node (i.e., subproblem), which is inefficient.
